sāhityadarpaṇe prathamaparicchedaḥ ||
oṃ namo gaṇeśāya |
avatarabikā |
granyārambhe nirvvighnena prāribhitaparisamāptikāmo vāṅmayā-
dhikṛtatayā vāgdevatāyāḥ sāmukhyamādhante ||
maṅgalācaraṇaṃ |
(1) śaradindusundararuciścetasi sā me girāṃ devī |
apatya tamaḥ santatamarthanakhilān prakāśayatu ||
asya granvaya kāvyāktayā kāvyaphalaireva phalavattvamiti ||
kāvyaphalānyāha |
(2) caturvvargaphalaprāptiḥ sukhāda padhiyāmapi |
kāvyādeva yatastena tatkharūpaṃ nirūpyate ||
turgaphalaprāpti kāvyato rāmādivat pravarttitavyaṃ na
rāvaṇādivadityādikṛtyākṛtyapravṛttinivṛtyupadeśadvāreṇa supra-
tītaiva | umañca |
“dharmārthakāmamotreṣu vaicacasyaṃ kalāsu ca |
karoti kontiṃ prītiñca sādhukāvyabhiṣevaṇamiti” |
kiñca | kāvyāṅkaprāptirbhagavannārāyaṇacaraṇāravindatavā-
dinā | ekaḥ śabdaḥ suprayuktaḥ samyagjñātaḥ svarge loke ca kāma-
sāhityadarpaye |
dhug bhavati ityādivedavākyebhyaca suprasiva | arthaprāptica pratya-
casiddhā| kāmaprāptikhārthadārava | molaprāptiyetajjanyadharmapha-
lānamusandhānāt| mocopayogivākye vyutpatvādhāyakatvāca |
caturvvargaprāptirhi vedaśāstrebhyo nīrasatayā duḥkhādeva pariṇata-
buddhīnāmeva jāyate| paramābhandasandohajanakatayā sukhādeva su-
kumārabuddhīnāmapi punaḥ kāvyādeva || nanu tarhi pariṇatabuddhibhiḥ
satsu vedaśāstreṣu kāvyeṣu kimiti yatnaḥ karaṇīya ityapi na
vakravyaṃ | kaṭukoṣadhopaśamanīyastha rogasya sitaśarkaropaśamanīyale
kasya vā rogiṇa: sitaśarkarāpravṛttiḥ sādhīyaso na syāt ||
kiñca | kāvyasyopādeyatvamāgneyapurāṇe'pyukaṃ |
"maratvaṃ durlabhaṃ loke vidyā tatra sudubhā |
kavitvaṃ durlabhaṃ tatra pratistatra sudurlabheti” |
nivargasādhanaṃ nāvyamiti ca viṣṇupurāṇe |
“kāvyālāpāca ye kecidgītakānyakhilāni ca |
śabdamūrttidharasyaite viṣyoraṃjā mahātmana iti” ||
tena hetunā tasya kāvyasya svarūpaṃ nirūpyate | etenābhidheyañca
pradarśitaṃ ||
tat kiṃsvarūpaṃ tāvatkāvyamityapecāvāṃ kañcit “tadadoṣau
śabdārtho saguṇāvamalaṅkṛtī punaḥ kāpīti” etacinyaṃ | tathāhi
yadi doṣarahitasyaiva kāvyalāṅgīkārasadā |
“nyakkāro jhayameva me yadarayastatrāpyaso tāpasa:
mo'pyacaiva nihanti rācasakulaṃ jīvatyo rāvaṇaḥ |
• prathamaparichedaḥ |
dhik dhik śakrajitaṃ prabodhitavatā kiṃ kumbhakarhena vā
svargagrāmaṭikāviluṭamasṛthocnaiḥ kimebhirbhujaiḥ” ||
śrasva lokasya vidheyāvimarṣadoṣaduṣṭatayā kāvyatvaṃ na syāt
pratyuta dhvanilemottama kāvyatā'sītā tasmādavyāptisaṃcala-
doṣaḥ || nanu kaccidevāṃśo'tra duṣṭo na punaḥ sarvve'poti cet
tarhi yatrāṃśe doṣaḥ mo'kāvyatvaprayojako yatra dhvaniḥ sa uttama-
kāvyatyaprayojaka ityaṃtrābhyāmubhayata prākṛvyamāṇaṃ kāvyamakāvyaṃ vā
kimapi na syāt || na ca kiñcidevāṃśaṃ kāvyasya dūṣayantaḥ śruti-
duṣṭādayo doṣāḥ kiṃ tarhi sarvvameva kāvyaṃ | tathāhi | kāvyātma-
bhūtasya rasasthānapakarṣakatve teṣāṃ doṣatvamapi nāṅgīkriyate | anyathā
nityadoṣānityadoṣatvabyavasthāpi na syāt || yadu dhvamilatā |
“śrutiduṣṭādayo doṣā śramityā ye ca darśitāḥ |
dhvanyātmanyeva ṭaṇāre te heyā ityudāhṛtāḥ” ||
kiñcaivaṃ kāvyaṃ praviralaviṣayaṃ nirvviṣayaṃ vā syāt | sarvvathā
nirdeṣasyaikāntamasambhavāt | nanu īṣadarthe mañaḥ prayoga iti cet
tarhi īvadoṣo śabdārthoṃ kāvyamitthurnirdeṣiyoḥ kāvyatvaṃ na
syāt| sati sambhave īṣaddoṣāviti ced etadapi kāvyalakṣaṇe
'vācyaṃ | ratnādilakṣaṇe koṭānuvedhādiparihāravat | na hi
kīṭānuvedhādayo ratnasya ratnatvaṃ vyāhantumīśāḥ kintu upādeya-
tāratamyameva karttu tadatra śrutiduṣṭādayo'pi kāvyasya || uktañca |
“kīṭāmuviṅgaratnādisādhārathena kāvyatā |
duṣṭeṣvapi matā yatra ramādyamugamaḥ sphuṭaḥ” ||
sāhityadarpaye|
kiñca | śabdārthayoḥ samusatyaviśeṣasamanupapatraṃ | gulānāṃ rasaikadha-
lakha"ye rasayāṅgino dharmaḥ praudaya vātmana” ityādinā
tenaiva pratipāditatvāt || rasāmiccañcakatlena upacārata upapadyata
iti cet tathāpyayukraṃ | tathāhi | taboḥ kāvyasvarūpatvenābhi
mataceoḥ śabdārthayo rameo'sti na vā | nāsti ced guruvalamapi
nāsti| guṇānāṃ tadanvayavyatirekānuvidhāyitvāt | asti cet
kathaṃ nobhaṃ rasavantāviti viśeṣaṇaṃ | guruvācānyathānupapattyā
etasabhyata iti cet tarhi sarasāvityeva vatuṃ yuktaṃ ma sagupāviti |
na hi prācimato deśā iti vakravye “baudimato deśā” iti
kejāṣṇucyate || nanu śabdārthai saguṇāvityanena gulābhivyañcako
śabdārthī kāvye prayovyāvityabhiprāya iti cet | na | gulābhi-
vyañcakaśabdārthabalasyāpi kāvya utkarṣamātrādhāvakatvaṃ na tu
svarūpādhāyakavaṃ| una hi "kāvyasya śabdārthī śarīraṃ rakhādi-
bātmā guṇāḥ praurvyādivad doṣāḥ kāṇatvādivad rītatheo'va-
yavasaṃsthānaviśeṣavad alaṅkārāḥ kaṭakakuṇḍasādivariti” |
etena “analaṅkṛtī puna: kāpīti” yadukraṃ tadapi parāsta | asya
artha: “samaṃtra sāsadvārī kacivasphuṭāladdhārāvapi śabdācī
kāvyamiti”| tatra sāladvāraśabdārthayorapi kāvya utkarṣamātrā-
dhāvakatvāt | etema “vakrokriḥ kāvyajīvitamiti” bakrośijīvi-
takāroktamapi parāstaṃ | vakrośerakhaddhārarūpatvāt| yaca kaci-
dasphuṭākhadārale udāhṛtaṃ |
“caḥ kaumāraharaḥ sa eva hi varastā eva caitracapām
prathamaparichedaḥ |
te comīkhitamālatīsurabhavaḥ prauḍhāḥ kadammānilāḥ |
sā caivāsmi tathāpi tatra suratavyāpāra solāvidhā
revārodhasi vetalītarutale cetaḥ samutkaṣṭhate" ||
etacinvaṃ| atra hi vibhāvanāviśeṣāktimūlasya sandehasa-
khāraphuṭavaṃ | etena |
"adoṣaṃ guṇavat kāvyamalaṅkārairalaṅkataṃ |
rasānvitaṃ kaviḥ kurvvan kīrtti prītiñca vindati” ||
ityādīnāmapi kāvya cañcalatvamapāsaṃ|| yattu dhvanikāraṇotaṃ
"kāvyakhātmā dhvaniriti” tat kiṃ vaslalaṅkāraramādilacara-
trirūpo dhvaṇiḥ kāvyasyātmā uta rasādirūpamātro vā | nādyaḥ |
prahekhikādāvativyāpteḥ | dvitīyacedomiti brūmaḥ || nanu badi
rasādimātrarūpa eva dhvaniḥ kāvyasyātmā tadā |
"yattā etva nimanjada etya ahaṃ divasa paloehi |
mā paritra ranticandhatra sevjāī maha nimajahisi” :
ityādī vastumājavyale kathaṃ kāvyavyavahāra iti ced
batrāpi rasābhāsavattathaiva grūmaḥ | anyathā “devadatto grāmaṃ yā-
tīti” vākye tamṛtyasya tadanusaraṇarūpavyaṅgyāvagaterapi kāvyatvaṃ
khāt | asmiti cet | na | rasavata eva kāvyatvāṅgīkārāt | kā-
vyastha prayojanaṃ hi rasāsvāda sukhapiṇḍadānadāreṇa | vedaśāstravi
saṃskṛtārthaḥ | śramūratra nimavyati patrāhaṃ divasa eva prajokaya |
mā pani rābandhana prabyāvāṃ mama nimaṃśasi | ṭīkā |
sāhityadarpaye |
mukhānāṃ sukumāramatīnāṃ rājaputraprabhṛtīnāṃ vineyānāṃ rāmā-
divat pravarttitavyaṃ na rāvaṇādivadityādikatyākatyapravṛttini-
tṛttyupadeśa iti cirantanairaputatvāt | tathā cāgnecapurāve'ṣṇutaṃ |
“vāmbedagdhyapradhāne'pi rasa evāca jīvitamiti” |
vyakti vivekakāreṇāpyu “kāvyasyātmani anini rasādirūpe
ma kasyaciddhimatiriti” | dhvanikārevāṇutaṃ | “na hi kaveri-
tivṛttamātranibāheṣvātmalābha itihāsādereva tatmiḍreri-
tyādi” || nanu tarhi prabandhāntabatināṃ keṣāmapi nīrasānāṃ
paścānāṃ kāvyatvaṃ na syāditi cet | na | rasavat paścāntargatanī-
rakhapadānāmiva padyarase prabandharasenaiva teṣāṃ rasavattāṅgīkā-
rāt | cattu nīrasembapi guṇābhivyacakavarṣasabhāvād doṣābhā-
vādalaṅkārasadbhāvāca kāvyavyavahāraḥ sa rasādimatkāvyabandha-
sāmyāhauṇa eva | yattu vāmanenokaṃ “rotirātmā kāvyasyeti"
tantra rīteḥ saṭṭanāviśeṣatvāt | saṅghaṭṭanāyāca avayavasaṃsthā-
narūpalāt | manaca tadbhivavāt | yaca dhvanikārelokaṃ |
“arthaḥ sahṛdayaścāṣyaḥ kāvyātmā yo vyavakhitaḥ |
vācyapratīyamānāsyo tasya bhedāvubhau stāviti” ||
tatra vācyasyātmatvaṃ "kāvyasyātmā dhvaniriti” svavacana-
viredhādevāpāstaṃ || tat kiṃ punaḥ kāvyamityucyate |
(3) vākyaṃ rasātmakaṃ kāvyaṃ
rasasvarūpaṃ nirūpayiṣyāmaḥ | rasa evātmā sārarūpatayā jīva-
nādhāyako'sya teja vinā tasya kāvyatvānaṅgīkārāt | raskhata iti
prathamaparichedaḥ |
rasa iti vyutpattiyogād bhāvatadābhāsādayo'pi gṛhyante |
tatra ramo yathā |
“5ṭhanyaṃ vāsagṛhaṃ vilokya śayanādutthāya kiñcicchanai-
niṃdrāvyājamupāgatasya suciraṃ niyaṃ patthurmukhaṃ |
viśrandhaṃ paricumya jātapula kāmālokya gaṇḍasthaloṃ
khajānavamukhī priyeṇa isatā bālā ciraṃ cumbitā” ||
hi sambhogaṭaṅgārākhyo rasaḥ || bhāvo yathā mahāpā-
narāghavānandasāndhivigrahikāṇāṃ |
“yasthācīyata praskasīvi jaladhiḥ pṛṣṭhe jaganmaṇḍa khaṃ
daṃḍrāyāṃ dharaṇī nakhe ditisutādhīśaḥ pade rodasī |
krodhe catragaṇaḥ bhare daśamukhaḥ pāṇī pralambāsuro
dhyāne viśvamasāvadhārmikakulaṃ kasmecidamai namaḥ” ||
atra bhagavaddiṣayā ratibhāvaḥ | rasābhāso yathā |
“madhudirephaḥ kusumaikapātre papau priyāṃ svāmanuvarttamānaḥ |
ejeca spardhanimīlitārthī mṛgīmakaṇḍūyata kṛṣṇasāraḥ” ||
atra hi sambhogaṇṭaṅgārasya tiryyagviṣayatvādramābhāsaḥ |
evamanyat || doṣāḥ punaḥ kāvye kiṃkharūpā ityucyate |
(4) doṣāstasyāpakarṣakāḥ |
śrutiduṣṭāpuṣṭārthavādayaḥ kāṇalakhañcatvādaya iva dehadāre-
deva śabdārthadāreṇa vyabhicāribhāvādaḥ svaśabdavā yatvādayo
mūrkhatādaya rava sākṣāt kāvyasyātmabhūtaṃ rasamapakarṣayantaḥ kāvya-
sāhiladarpaye|
sthāpakarṣakā uccante | eva viśeṣodāharabāni baccāmaḥ ||
gurugarayaḥ kiṃ svarūpā ityucyate |
(5) utkarṣadetavaḥ proktā guṇālaṅkārarītayaḥ ||'
guṇāḥ naumyādivat | asahārāḥ kaṭakabruṣḍalādivat |
rītayo'vayavasaṃsthānaviśeṣavat | dehadāreṇeva śabdārthadāreṇa tameva
kāvyasthātmabhūtarasamutkarṣaṃyantaḥ kāvyasthotkarṣakā uccante |
iha yadyapi guṇānāṃ rasadhalaṃ tathāpi guṇaśabdo'ca guṇābhi-
vyaccakaśabdārthayorupacarmyate | ataca guṇābhivyañcakāḥ śabdā
rasasyotkarṣakā ityukaṃ bhavatīti prāgevokaṃ | eṣāmapi viśeṣā-
dāharaṇāni vacyāmaḥ || * ||
iti śrīmannārāyaṇacaraṇāravindamadhuvratasāhityārḍavakarṇa -
dhāradhvaniprasthāpanaparamā cārthakavisvatiratnākarāṣṭrājabhāṣā-
vāravilāsinībhujaṅgasānbhivigrahikamahāpātraśrīviśvanātha kavi-
rājakatā sāhityadarpaye kāvyasvarūpanirūpaṇo nāma prathamaḥ
paricchedaḥ || * ||
vākyasvarūpamāha |
(6) vākyaṃ syādyogyatākāṅkṣāsattiyuktaḥ padoccayaḥ ||
yogyatā padārthānāṃ parasparasambandhe vādhābhāvaḥ | padoṣa-
yasyaitadabhāve'pi vākyale vahinā siñcatītyapi vākyaṃ syāt |
trākāṅkṣā pratītiparyavasānavirahaḥ | sa ca śroturjiśābāva-
rūpaḥ| mirākāṅkṣasya vākyale gauravaḥ puruṣo istītyādīnā-
mapi vākyatvaṃ syāt | āvattirbuddhavicchedaḥ | buddhivicchede'pi
hitovapariveśa |
vākyale idānīmucaritasya devadattaśabdasya dināntarocāri-
tena gacchatītipadena saṅgatiḥ syāt | catrākāṅkṣā yogyatabo-
rāmārthadharmatve'pi padocavadharmalamupacārāt ||
(7) vākyoccayo mahāvākyam |
yogyatākāṅkṣāsattiyukta eva |
(8) ityaṃ vākyaṃ dvidhā mataṃ |
ityamiti vākyalena mahāvākyalena ca || utañca |
“svārthabodhasamāptānāmaṅgāṅgitvavyapecavā |
vākyānāmekavākyatvaṃ puna: saṃhatya jāyata iti” |
tatra vākyaṃ yathā | “śūmyaṃ vāsagṛhamityādi" | mahāvākyaṃ
thathā | rāmāyaṇamahābhārataraghuvaṃśādi || padoṣayo vākyami-
vyukaṃ tat kiṃ padakhacaṇamityata āī|
(8) varṣmīḥ padaṃ prayogāśananvitaikārthabodhakāḥ ||
yathā ghaṭaḥ | prayogāhī iti prātipadikasya vyavacchedaḥ |
ananviteti vākyamahāvākyabo: | eketi sākāmānekapadavā-
yānāṃ | arthabodhakā iti kacaṭatapānāṃ | varṣā iti bahuvaca-
namavivacitaṃ | tatra
(10) arthī vāccaca lakṣyasa vyayaśceti vidhā mataḥ |
eṣāṃ varupamāha |
(11) bācceo'rthe
'bhidhayā bodhyo lakṣyo lakṣaṇayā mataḥ ||
vyo vyavanayā tāḥ syustisraḥ śabdasya śaktayaḥ |
sāhityadarpayeṃ|
tā abhidhāyāḥ| tatra saṅketitārthasya bodhanādagrimā'bhidhā |
uttamavṛddhena madhyamahaṅmuhima “māmānaya” ityu taṃ gavānaya-
napravṛttamupalabhya bālo'sya vākyasya “sāsādimatpiṇḍānacana-
marthaḥ" iti prathamaṃ pratipadyate | anantaraca "gāṃ badhāna aśvamā-
naya” ityādāvāvāpoṅkārābhyāṃ gośabdasya "bāzādimānarthaḥ”
grāmavanaśabdala cāharaṇamarthaḥ” iti matamavadhārayati |
kacitra prasiddhūpadasamabhivyāhārāt | bathā|
“iha prabhitrakamalodare madhūni madhukaraḥ pibati” itya
kacidāptopadeśāt| yathā “śrayamaśvaśabdavācca" ityatra | tañca
saaititamarthaṃ bodhayantī śabdasya prāntarājantaritā prakrirabhidhā
nāma ||
(12) sahato te jāto guṇadravyakriyāsu ca ||
jātirgaipicchādiṣu gotvādikā | guṇeo viśeṣādhāmahetuḥ
siddho vastudharmaḥ | śakkādayo hi gavādikaṃ sajātīyebhyaḥ
kṛṣṇagavādibhyo vyāvarttayanti | draṣyabhandā ekavyaktivācino
hariharaṅitthaḍavitvādayaḥ | kriyāḥ sādhyarūpā vastudhamaḥ pā
kādathaḥ | eṣa hi adhiśrayalāvatrayaṇācantādipūrvīparībhūta-
vyāpārakalāpa: pākādiśabdavācyaḥ | embeva hi vyakrerapādhiṣu
sato te navyau | mānanya vyabhicāradoṣāpātāt ||
atha lakṣaṇā |
(13) mukhyārthavādhe tayukto yayānyo'rthaḥ pratīyate |
rūḍhaḥ prayojanādāsau lakṣaṇā śaktirarpitā ||
dvitīyaparichedaḥ |
“kaliṅgaḥ sāhasikaḥ” ityādī kaliṅgādiśabdo deśaviśe-
bādirūpe svārtha'sambhavan yayā śabdaya pratlā svasaṃyutān puru-
pādīn pratyācavati | yathā ca "gaṅgāyāṃ ghoṣaḥ” ityādī gaṅgā-
diśabdo jasamavādirūpārthavācakatvāt prakṛte'sambhavam svastha sā-
mopyādisambandhasambandhinaṃ taṭādiṃ bodhayati sā śabdavārpitā
svābhāviketarā īśvarānugāvitā vā praticalā nāma |
pūrvvatra heva rūḍhiḥ prasiddhireva| uttaraja “gaṅgātaṭe ghoṣaḥ” iti
pratipādanādasabhyasya śītalapāvanatvātiśayasya bodhanarūpaṃ prayo-
banaṃ | hetuṃ vināpi yasya kasyacit sambandhino lakṣaṇe'tiprasaṅgaḥ
sthādityata uktaṃ| “rūḍheḥ prayojanādvāpīti”| kecit tu "karmaṇi
kuśalaḥ” iti rūḍhābudāharanti | teṣāmayamabhiprāyaḥ "kubhaṃ
khāti” iti vyutpattilabhyaḥ kutragrāhirūpo mukhyo'rthaḥ prakṛte'sambha-
vam vivecakatvasādhamaryasambandhasambandhinaṃ dakṣarūpamarthaṃ bodhavati |
tadanye na manyo | kuśagrāhirūpārthastra vyutpattilabhyatve'pi
datrarūpasyaiva mukhyārthatvāt| anyaddhi śabdānāṃ vyutpattinimitta-
manyaca pravṛttinimittaṃ| vyutpattisabhyasya mukhyārthale “gāḥ śete”
ityatrāpi khacavā syāt | “gamerḍer” iti gamadhātorḍepratyayena,
vyutpāditasya gośabdasya śayanakāle'pi prayogāt ||
tadbhedānāha |
(14) mukhyārthasthetarājepo vākyārthe'nvayasiddhaye |
syādātmano'pyupādānādeṣopādānalakṣaṇā ||
rūḍhāvupādānalacyā yathā “veto bhāvati” | prabojane yathā
sāhityadarpaye|
“kuntāḥ praviśanti” | anayorhi śvetādibhiḥ kuntādibhiya
acetagatathā kevalebhāvanapraveśanakriyayoḥ kartṛtathā'nvayamalabha
mānairetasiddhaye ātmasambandhio'yādayaḥ puruṣārayavāci
pyante | pūrvvatra prayojanābhāvāṭūḍiḥ | uttaratra kuntādīnā-
matigahanatvaṃ prayojanaṃ | atra ca mukhyārthasyātmano'pyupādānaṃ |
sacapakhalapāṣāntu parasyaivopalacapamityanaborbhedaḥ | inameva
"aba itvācī" ityucyate ||

'nvaṣaṣiddhaye kaciṅgagaṅgāśabdāvātmānamarpayataḥ || yathā vā |
“upacataṃ bahu tatra kimucyate sujanatā prathitā bhavatā paraṃ |

(17) viṣayasyānigīrṣasthānyatādā byaprītikṛt |

(18) sāhaśyetara sambandhāḥ śuddāstāḥ sakalā api ||

hemante sukhāni ” || prayojane yathā | “rājakumārā gacchanti” ||

prayojane yathā | “gorjaspati” |
atra kecidāhuḥ “gosahacāriṇo guṇā jāyamānyādayo

bhavanti” | tadyutaṃ | gośabdasyā gṛhītasaṅketaṃ vāhīkārthamabhidhā-
tumasāmarthyāt | gośabdārthamātrabodhanācca | abhidhāyā vira-
tatvād viratāyāca punarutthānābhāvāt | anye punargośabdena
“vāhīkāthī nābhidhīyate kintu svārthasahacāriguṇa sājatyena

hyatra gośabdādā hīkārthaḥ pratīyate na vā | ādye'pi gośabdā-
dvitīyaparichedaḥ |
deva vā lacitādā guṇāt | avinābhāvadvārā tatra na prathamaḥ |
vāhīkārthe'syāsaṅketitatvāt | na dvitīyaḥ | avinābhāvalabhya-

prapūte || na dvitīyaḥ | yadi hi gośabdādvāhīkārthe na pratī-
cate tadāsya vāhīkasya sāmānādhikarathamasamañjasaṃ syāt | tasmā-
datra gośabdo mukhyayā vṛttyā vāhīkaśabdena sahānvayamalabhamā-
mo'jñatvādisādhamaryasambandhādāhīkārthaṃ lacayati | vāhīkasyā-
śalādyatiśayameodhanaṃ prayojanaṃ | iyañca guṇayogād “goṣṇī”
ratyucyate | pūrvī tu upacārāmiśraṇāt śaddhā | upacāro hi

pratītiḥ | tasmādevamādiṣu śava khacaṇā ||
(18) vyaṅgyasya gūḍhāgūḍhatvād dvidhā syuḥ phalalakṣaṇāḥ ||

ke'pyasahamānā trāḥ | “svaro'pi kākādirūpaḥ kāvye vi

veti” | tatra | kharāḥ kākādaya udāttādayo vā vyaya-

naṅgīkārapravaṅgaḥ | na ca tathā | ata evāja: "ślevanirūpaṇa-
prastāve kābamārge slaro na gasthata iti ca navaḥ” ityalamupajī-
byānāṃ mānyānāṃ vyākhyāneṣu kaṭācamicepeṇa | āādiśabdād

bathā mama tātapādānāṃ mahāpācacaturhaśabhāṣāvilāsinībhu

tatmācalaccāmavṛddhaprapitāmahasahṛdayagoṣṭhīgariṣṭhakavipaḍitamu
smaśrīmannāvacapādainaṃ | tadāha dharmadattaḥ svagranthe |
“rase sāraṇamatkāraḥ sarvatrāpyanubhūvate |
tacamatkārasārale sarvvanāpyaguto rasaḥ |
tasmādadbhutamevāha kṛtī nārāyaco rasamiti” ||
keciditi prāśanapuṇatrātibhiḥ || yadu | “puṇavantaḥ
pramisanti bogivadrasasamātimiti” || yadyapi “khādaḥ kā
bārthavanbhedādātmānandalamuhavaḥ" ityukradiśā rasāsvādāna-
sāhityadarpaye|
tirilamuāṃ tathāpi “rasaḥ khādyate” iti kālpanikaṃ bhedamu-
rarīkṛtya karma katari vā prayogaḥ || tadukraṃ | “rakhamānatāmātra-
bāratvāt prakāśaśarīrādajanya eva hi rakha: " iti | evamanba-
pāpyevaṃvidhaskhaleṣūpacāreca prayomā śeṣāḥ | janyetāvatā rasa-
yācavamutaṃ bhavatīti vyañjanāvāca mānaviśeṣatvād dabo-
kāmāpatitaṃ | tataya |
“strajñānenānyadhīhetuḥ bi'rthe vyañcako mataḥ |
bathā dīpo'nyathābhāve ko viśeṣo'sya kārakāt" | ityumana-
diśā ghaṭapradīpavad vyaṅgāvyaprakaboḥ pārthakyameveti kathaṃ ramaṇa
vyaṅgyateti cet satyamuktaṃ | ata evāḥ | vijaṣaṇa evāyaṃ kṛti-
aptibhedebhyaḥ slādanāsyaḥ katiyāpāraḥ | ata eva hi rakhanā-
sthādagacamatkaraṇādayo vicaṣaṇa eva vyapadeśā iti abhi
bhādivisacacavyāpāratātra prasādhanagrahisarassābhī rakhādīnāṃ
vyavamuktaṃ bhavatīti || manu tāī karuṇādīnāṃ rasānāṃ duḥkha-
mathatvādrasatvaṃ na syādityucyate |
(35) karuṇādāvapi rase jāyate yat paraṃ sukhaṃ |
sacetasāmanubhavaḥ pramāṇaṃ tatra kevalaṃ ||
ādiśabdādībhatsa bhayānakādayaḥ || tathāpyasahṛdayānāṃ mukha-
mudraṇāya pacāntaramucyate |
(36) kiśca teṣu yadā duḥkhaṃ na ko'pi syāttadunmukhaḥ |
na hi kaścit bacetā ātmano duḥkhāya pravarttate | karuṇādiṣu
sakalasthāpi sābhiniveśa pravṛttidarśanāt || anupapattyanta ramāī|
kṛtīyaparichedaḥ |
(36) tathā rāmāyaṇādīnāṃ bhavitā duḥkhahetutā ||
karuṇarasasya duḥkhahetutvāt karuṇarasapradhāna rāmāyaṇādi-
prabandhānāmapi duḥkhahetulaprasaṅgaḥ syāt || manu kathaṃ duḥkhakāra-
ṣebhya: sukhotpattirityāi |
(37) cetutvaṃ śokacaṣadargatebhyo lokasaṃśrayāt |
śokacarṣādayo loke jāyantāṃ nāma laukikāḥ ||
alaukikavibhāvatvaṃ prāptebhyaḥ kāvyasaṃśrayāt |
mukhaṃ sañjāyate tebhyaḥ sarvvebhyo'pīti kā kṣatiḥ ||
ye khalu rāmavanavāsādayo loke "duḥkhakāraṇāmi” ityu -
canme ta eva hi kāvyanāvyasamarpitā alaukikavibhāvanavyā-
pāravantayā kāraṇaśabdavācyatvaṃ vihāyālā kikavibhāvaśabdavā-
cavaṃ bhavante | tebhyaca surate dantaghātādibhya iva sukhameva
jāyate | ataca “laukikaśo ka irṣādikāraṇebhyo laukikaśo-
kaharṣādayo jāyante” iti loka eva pratiniyamaḥ | kāvye punaḥ
"sarvvebhyo'pi vibhāvādibhyaḥ sukhameva jāyate” iti niyamātra
kacciddovaḥ || kathaṃ tarhi haricandrādicaritasya kāvyanāvya-
yorapi darśanaśravaṇābhyāmaśrupātādayo jāyanta ityucyate |
(38) aśrupātādayastatatvāccetaso matāḥ |
tarhi kathaṃ kāvyataḥ sarveṣāmośī rasābhivyakirna jāyata
ityāha |
sāhityadarpaye|
(38) na jāyate tadā sādo binā ratyādivāsanāṃ ||
bāsanā cedānīmanī prātavī rasāsvādahetuḥ | tatra
badi āyā na syānojiyajaramomāṃsakādīnāmapi vā syāt |
yadi dvitīyā na syād cadrāgiṇāmapi keṣācidra mododho na
dṛśyate tantra syāt || utañca dharmadattena |
"savāsamānāṃ sabhyānāṃ rasaskhāsvādanaṃ bhavet |
nibāsanāstu raṅgāntaḥkāṣṭhakuyāmabavibhāḥ” ||
iti || nanu kathaṃ rāmādiratyāyudodhakāraṇaiḥ sītādibhiḥ
sāmāṇikaratyāyuddodha ityucyate |
(40) vyāpāro'sti vibhāvādenīyā sādhāraṇī kṛtiḥ|
tatprabhāṣena yasyāsan pāyodhiśavanādayaḥ ||
pramātā tadabhedena khātmānaṃ pratipadyate |
manu kathaṃ manuṣyamāṣasya samudralaṅamādābutsāhododha ityu
cate |
(42) usācādisamuddodhaḥ sādhāraṇyābhimānataḥ ||
nṛṇāmapi samudrādilaṅghanādau na duṣyati |
ratyādayo'pi sādhāraṇyena pratoyanta ityāha |
(42) sādhāraṇyena ratyādirapi taddat pratīyate ||-
ratyāderapi hyātmagatatvena pratīto sabhyānāṃ brīḍāsaṅgādi-
rbhavet paragataleja varakhatāpātaḥ || vibhābādayo'pi prathamataḥ
sādhāraṣyena pratīyanta ityāha |
tīvaradaḥ|
(43) parakha na parasyeti mameti na mameti ca ||
tadākhāde vibhāvādaḥ parichedona vidyate|
manu tathāpi kathamevamalaukikalameteṣāṃ vibhāṣādīnāmi-
tyucyate |
(44) vibhāvanādivyāpāramalaukikamupeyuṣa ||
alaukikatvameteṣāṃ bhūṣaṇaṃ na tu dūṣaṇaṃ |
śrādiśabdādamubhāvanasañcārale | tatra vibhāvanaṃ ratyādevi -
śeṣeṇāsvādākuraṇayogyatānavarga || anubhāvanamevambhūtasya ratyādeḥ
samajantarameva rasādirūpatayā bhāvanaṃ || sañcāraṇaṃ tathābhūtasyai
tasya sambakucāraṇaṃ || vibhāvādīnāṃ pathāsa kāraṇakāsa-
ikāritve kathaṃ trayāṇāmapi rasododhe kāra catvamityucyate |
(45) kāraṇaṃ kāryyasañcāri rūpā api hi lokataḥ ||
rasoddodhe vibhāvādyāḥ kāraṇānyeva te matāḥ |
nanu tarhi kathaṃ rasāskhāde teṣāmekaḥ pratibhāsa ityucyate |
(49) pratīyamānaḥ prathamaṃ pratyekaṃ ceturucyate ||
tataḥ sambalitaḥ savvā vibhāvādiḥ sacetasāṃ |
prapānakarasanyāyāñcarvyamāṇo raso bhavet ||
yathā khaṇḍamaricādīnāṃ sammelanādapūrṇa iva kaccidāsvādaḥ
prapānakarase sañcāvate vibhāvādi samesavādihāpi tathetyarthaḥ ||
manu yadi vibhāvānubhāvavyabhicāribhikilitaireva rasastat kathaṃ
ekasya dayovī badbhāve'pi sa khādityute |
sāhityadarpaye|
(47) saṅgāvaceḍibhāvādeīyārekasya vā bhavet |
jhaṭityanyasamākṣepe tadā doṣo na vidyate ||
anyasamātrepaca prakaraṇādivaśāt | yathā |
“dīrghācaṃ gharadindukānti vadanaṃ vāha natāvaṃsayoḥ
saṅkṣiptaṃ niviḍovatastanamuraḥ pārśve prasṛṣṭe va
madhyaḥ pācimito nitamni jaghanaṃ pādāvudayāṅgulī
indo narttacituryathaiva manasaḥ sṛṣṭaṃ tathāsyā vapuḥ” ||
atra mālavikāmabhikhavato'gnimitrasya mālavikārūpavi-
bhāvamājavarṣane sañcāriṇāmautsukyādīnāmanubhāvānāñca jaya-
navisphārādīnāmaiaucityādevācepaḥ | evamanyāce pe'pyūaṃ || “prabhu -
kārgyagato rasaḥ” iti vadataḥ pratyāha |
(48) pārimityā laukikatvāt sāntarāyatayā tathā |
anukāryyasya ratyāderuddodho na raso bhavet ||
sītādidarśanādijo rāmādiratyāyuddhodho hi parimito
laukiko nāvyakāvyadarśanādeḥ sāntarāyaśca | tasmāt kathaṃ
rasarūpatāmiyāt | ramasyaita citayavilakṣaṇadharmākatvāt |
anukartagartalañcāsya nirasyati |
kiñca
(58) śikṣābhyāsādimātreṇa rāghavādaḥ kharūpatāṃ |
darśayan narttako naiva rasasyāsvādako bhavet ||
(50) kāvyārthabhāvanenāyamapi sabhyapadāspadaṃ |
tṛtīyaparichedaḥ |
badi punarmaṭo'pi kāvyārthabhāvanayā rāmādirūpatāmātmano
darśayet tadā mo'pi sabhyamadhya eva gacyate |
(51) nāyaṃ jñāpyaḥ khasattāyāṃ pratītyavyabhicārataḥ ||
yo hi jñāpyo ghaṭādiḥ sa sannapi kadācidajñāto bhavati
na iyaṃ tathā | pratītimantareṣābhāvāt ||
(52) yasmādeṣa vibhāvādisamūcālambanātmakaḥ |
tasmānna kāryyaḥ
yadi rasaḥ kāryyaḥ syāt tadā vibhāvādijñānakāraṇaka
eva syāt| tatacca ramapratītikāle vibhāvādayo na pratīyeran |
kāraṇajjñānatatkāryyajjñānayoryugapadadarśanāt | na hi candamaspa-
jñānaṃ tavjanyasukhajñānaṃ caikadā sambhavati | ramasva vibhāvā-
disamūhālambanātmaka tathaiva pratīterna vibhāvādijñānakāracaka-
lamityabhiprāyaḥ ||
(53) no nityaḥ pūrvvasaṃvedanojjhitaḥ ||
asaṃvedanakāle cina bhāvo'pyasya vidyate |
ma khaṣu nityasya vastu mo'saṃvedanakā khe'sambhavo nāpi bhavi-
sthan | cācādānandamayasvaprakāśarūpatvāt | kāryyajñāpyavilaca-
prabhāvān jo varttamāno'pi ||
(54) vibhāvādiparāmarśaviṣayatvāt sacetasāṃ ||
parānandamayatvena saṃvedyatvādapi sphuṭaṃ |
sāhityadarpaye |
na nirvikalpakaṃ jñānaṃ tasya grāhakamiṣyate ||
tathā' bhilāpasaṃsargayogyatvabirāna ca |
sakkimpakasaṃvedyaḥ
savikalpaka jñāna saṃvedyānāṃ hi vacanaprayogayogyatā na tu
ramasya | tathā |
sākṣātkāratayā na ca ||
parokṣastatprakāśo nāparokṣaḥ śabdasambhavāt |
tat kathaya kīdṛgasya tavaṃ || evamaśrutādṛṣṭapūrvvanirūpaṇapra-
kārasyetyāha |
(56) tasmādalaukikaḥ satyaṃ vedyaḥ sahṛdayairayaṃ ||
tat kiṃ puna: pramāṇaṃ tasya sadbhāva ityāha |
(57) pramāṇaṃ carvvaṇaivātra svābhinne viduṣā̭ mataṃ |
carvvaṇā''svādanaṃ | taca "svādaḥ kāvyārthasambhedādātmā-
mandasamudbhavaḥ” ityuktaprakāraṃ || manu yadi raso na kāryyastat kathaṃ
maharṣiṇā "vibhāvānubhāvavyabhicārisaṃyogādrasaniṣpattiḥ
iti lakṣaṇaṃ kṛtamityucyate |
(58) niṣpatyā carvvaṇasyāsya niṣpattirupacārataḥ |
yadyapi rasābhinnatayā carvvaṇasyāpi na kāryyatvaṃ tathāpi
tasya kādācitkatayā upacaritena kāryyavena kāryyala mupa caryyate ||
(59) avācyatvādikaṃ tasya kye vyañjanarūpaṇe |
tasya ramasya | ādiśabdādarācyavādi | namu yadi
99tṛtīyaparichedaḥ |
militā ratyādayo rasāt kathamasya prakāśatvaṃ kathaṃ vā'khaṇḍa-
vamityāha |
(60) ratyādi jīnatādātmyādeva yasmādraso bhavet ||
ato'sya svaprakāśatvamakhaṇḍatvañca siddhyati |
yadi ratyādikaṃ prakāśaśarīrādatiriktaṃ syāt tadaivāsya
svaprakāśatvaṃ na siyet| na tathā| tādātmyāṅgīkārāt | yadu |
"yadyapi ravānanyatayā carmaṇā na kāryya tathāpi kādācit-
katayā kāryyatvamupakalya tadekātmanyanādivāsanāpariṣatirūpe
ratyādibhāme'pi vyavahāraḥ” iti | “sukhāditādātyāṅgīkāre
cāsmākīṃ siddhāntabhayyāmadhiśayya divyavarṣasahasraṃ pramodanidrā-
mupeyāḥ” iti ca | “abhinno'pi sa pramātrā vāsanopanītaratyā-
ditādātmyagocarīkṛtaḥ” iti ca | jñānasya svaprakāśatvamanaṅgī
kurvvatāmupari vedāntibhireva nipātanīyo daṇḍaḥ | tādākyāde-
vāsyā khaṇḍatvaṃ | ratyādayo hi prathamamekaikaśa: pratīyamānāḥ sa
'pyekībhūtāḥ sphuranta eva rakhatāmāpadyante || taduktaṃ |
"vibhāvā anubhāvāsa sāttvikā vyabhicāriṇaḥ |
pratīyamānāḥ prathamaṃ khaṇḍo yānyakhaṇḍatāṃ” ||
iti | “paramārthatakhakhaṇḍa evācaṃ vedāntaprasiṅbrahmata cavadvedi-
tabya iti ca” || atha ke te vibhāvānubhāvavyabhicāriṇa itya-
pecāyāṃ vibhāvamāi |
((1) ratyāsuddodhakā loke vibhāvāḥ kāvyanāvyayoḥ ||
the hi loke rāmādigataratihāsādījāmuddodhakārayāni
sāhityadarpabe |
sotādavasa eva kāvye māye
niveśitāḥ santo "vibhāṣyante
trāsvādākuraprādurbhāvayogyāḥ kriyante sāmājikaratyādibhāvā
ebhiḥ” iti vibhāvā uccante || tadukraṃ bhartṛhariṇā |
“śabdopahitarūpāṃstām buddherviṣayatāṃ gatān |
pratyacāniva kaṃsādīn sādhanatvena manyate” ||
iti | tadbhedāvāha |
(62) ālambanoddīpanākhyau tasya bhedāvubhau mṛtau ||
spaṣṭaṃ || taca |
(63) ālambanaṃ nāyakādistamālambya rasogamāt |
śrādiśabdānnāyikāpratināyakādayaḥ | aba yo bastra vibhāvaḥ
sa tatsvarūpavarṇane vakṣyate || tatra nāyakaḥ |
(64) tyāgī kṛtī kulīnaḥ suśrīko rūpayauvanotsāhī ||
dakṣo'nuraktalokastejovaidagdhyaśīlavān netā |
daccaḥ ciprakārī | śīlaṃ sadvṛttaṃ | evamādiguṇasampano netā
nāyako bhavati || tadbhedānāha |
(65) dhīrodātto dhorodvatastathā dhīra lalita ||
dhīrapraśānta ityayamuktaḥ prathamaścaturbhedaḥ |
spaṣṭaṃ ca dhīrodātta: |
(66) avikatthanaḥ kṣamāvānatigambhīro mahāsattvaḥ ||
stheyān nigūḍhamānā dhīrodātto dṛḍhavrataḥ kathitaḥ |
avikatyano'nātmacāghākaraḥ | mahāsattvo harṣaśokācana-
tṛtīyaparichedaḥ |
bhibhūtasyabhāvaḥ | nigūḍhamāneo vinayacchannagargya: | dṛḍhavato'ṅgī-
kṛtaniṣṇakaḥ | yathā rāmayudhiṣṭhirādiḥ || atha dhīroddhataḥ |
(67) māyāparaḥ pracaṇḍaścapalo'haṅkāradarpabhūyiṣṭhaḥ ||
ātmāghānirato dharadharīdvataḥ kathitaḥ |
cathā bhīmamenādiḥ || atha dhīralakhitaḥ |
(68) niścintā mṛduraniśaṃ kalāparo dhīralalitaḥ syāt ||
kathā nṛtyādikā | yathā ratnāvasyādau vambarājādiḥ || atha
dhīrapraśāntaḥ |
(68) sāmānyaguṇairbhūyān dvijādiko dhorapraśāntaḥ syāt |
yathā mālatīmādhavādī mādhavādiḥ || eṣāñca bhṛṅgārirūpale
bhedānāi |
(70) ebhirddaciṇadhṛṣṭānukūlaśaṭharūpibhistu ṣoḍaśadhā |
tava teṣāṃ dhīrodātādīnāṃ pratyekaṃ dakṣiṇaṣṭaṣṭānukūlajaṭhaleja
boḍaaprakāro nāyakaḥ ||
(71) eṣu tvanekamahilāsa samarāgo dakṣiṇaḥ kaścitaḥ ||
hṛyosnicatuḥprasṭatiṣu nāthikāsu tulyānurāmo dakṣiṇamāyakaḥ |
pathā |
"khātā tiṣṭhati kuntaleśvarasutā vāro'ṅgarājasva sur
cūtairrājiriyaṃ jitā kamalabā devo prasācāca ca |
ityantaḥpurasundarīḥ prati malā vijñāya vijñāpite
devebā'pratipattimūḍhamanasā dināḥ khitaṃ nāḍikā:” #
khālida
(72) kṛtāgā api niḥśahastarvvito'pi na laccitaḥ |
rahadoṣo'pi mithyāvāk kathito bhṛṣṭanāyakaḥ ||
bacā mama |
"śoṣaṃ bīca murkha viṣummitumahaṃ bātaḥ samīpaṃ tataḥ
pādena prahataṃ tathā sapadi taṃ tvā bahāne mavi |
kiñcit tatra vidhātumacamatacā vāṣpaṃ tyajandhāḥ sakhe
dhyānacetasi kautukaṃ vitanute kopo'pi vāmabhuvaḥ” ||
(73) anukūla ekanirataḥ
hakalyāmeva nāyikāyāmāsako'nukūlanāvakaḥ | yathā |
“asmākaṃ sakhi vāsasī na rucira graiveyakaṃ mojvalaṃ
mo vakā gatiruitaṃ na hasitaṃ naivāsti kañcinmadaḥ |
kinlanthe'pi banā vadanti subhago'pyasyāḥ priyo nānyato
dṛṣṭiṃ micipatīti viśvamiyatā manyāmahe duḥkhitaṃ” ||
śaṭho'yamekaca bhāvo yaḥ
darśitavacira nurāgo vipriyamanyana gūḍhamācarati ||
caḥ pumarekasyāmeva nāyikāyāṃ baddhabhāvo iyorapi nāyi-
kathorvatrirdarśitānurāgo'nyasyāṃ nāyikāyāṃ gūḍhaṃ vipriyamāca-
rati sa bhaṭhanāyakaḥ | yathā |
“maṭho'nyasyāḥ kāñcīmaṇiracita mākarṇya sahasā
yadācisyameva praśithilabhujagrandhirabhavaḥ |
tadetat kācacye ghṛtamadhumayatvada javaco-
viṣecāghūrvanto kimapi na sakhī me gaṇayati”|
tīvaparichedaḥ |
(75) eṣāśca naividhyāt sarveṣāmuttamamadhyābhamatyena |
uktā nāyakabhedācatvāriṃśattathā'ṭo ca ||
sarvvevāmuktaṣoḍaśabhedānāṃ || atha prasaṅgādetevāṃ bahāyānāi |
(76) dūrāvarttini syāt tasya prāsaniketivṛtte tu |
kiñcittadguṇahīnaḥ sacāya evāsya pīṭhamaīsthiḥ ||
tastra nāyakasya bajavyāpini prasaṅgasaṅgate itivṛtte'nantaro-
bhaiyakasāmānyaguṇaiḥ kiñcidūnaḥ pīṭhamaīnāmā sahāyo bhavati |
vathā rāmacandrādīnāṃ sugrīvādayaḥ || atha śṛṅgāramahāvāḥ |
(77) pūṭanārasya sāyā viṭaceṭavidūṣakādyāḥ syuḥ |
bhaktā narmāta nipuṇāḥ kupitavadhūmānabhañjanāḥ śuddāḥ ||
trādiśabdānmālākārarajakatāmbūlika gāndhikādayaḥ|| tatra viṭaḥ|
(78) sambhogahīnasampad vista dhūrttaḥ kasaikadeśabhaḥ |
beśopacārakuśalo vāmmī madhuro'tha bahumato goṣyāṃ ||
ceṭa: prasiddha eva |
(78) kusumavasantādyabhiṣaḥ kavapurveśabhāṣādyaiḥ |
hāsyakaraḥ kalacaratirvidūṣakaḥ syāt svakarmaṇaḥ ||
svakarma bhojanādi | arthacintanamahāyamāī |
(80) mantrī syādarthacintāyāmarthāstantrāvapādayaḥ |
calana mahāyakathanaprastāve “mantrī svaṃñcobhayañcāpi sā

sāhibadabaṃḍe |

tasyārthacintane” iti kenacilavaṇaṃ kṛtaṃ tadapi rājeo'rthacimā-

kasya | “arthacimāne mantrī bahāyaḥ" ityumo'pi nāyakasvārthata
eva bilāt| cadakaṃ "janmiyā sacitaḥ trevā manliṣvā-
battaviyaḥ” iti • tadapi cāṇakathanenaiva dhīralakhitaka
malimacāyattārthacintanopapatermatārthaṃ | na cārthacintane tasya

paramātmasvarūpaṃ vā tasyālambanamiṣyate |
puṇyāśramacaritīrtharamyavanādayaḥ ||
mahāpuruṣasaṅgādyāstasyoddīpanarūpiṇaḥ |
cathā|

romāścādyāvānubhāṣāstathā syurvyabhicāriṇaḥ ||
nirbeda varṣāraṇamatibhūtadayādayaḥ |
"rajyāntavaratastathā dhṛtajaratkanyākhavastrādhvagaiḥ

niḥśaṅkaṃ karaṭaḥ kadā karapuṭobhicāṃ viśuṣṭhiṣyati” ||
puṣṭistu mahābhāratādī draṣṭavyā ||

“na yatra duḥkhaṃ na sukhaṃ na cintā na dveṣarāgaunaca kācidi chā|

abe gaurīnātha tripurahara śambho cinayana
prasodeti krośannimiṣamiva mevyāmi divasām” ||

vyāpāditāceti samucayālaṅkāro vyaṅgyaḥ ||
" sajjera sura himāso ra āpale juna janasalama he |
ahilaasahatrāramuhe chatrapasavapattaye amaṅgasya śare”*||
vasantaḥ śarakāraḥ kāmo dhanvo yuvatavo khacyaṃ puṣpāṇi
śarā iti kaviprauḍhoktisiddhaṃ vastu prakāśībhavādanavijṛmbhaṇarūpaṃ
vastu vyanaki ||
* sabjei iti | sabjayati surabhimāso na cānayavi yuvatijana-
lakṣyasahān | abhinavasahakāramukhām navapakṣa va patanāmaṅgasya
śarān | iti saṃ0 | ṭī0 ||
caturthaparichedaḥ |
"rajanīṣu vimalabhāno: karajālena prakāśitaṃ vīra |
dhavalayati bhuvanamaṇḍalamakhilaṃ taba kīrttisantatiḥ satataṃ”||
catra kavi prauḍhophisi ḍrena vastunā korttisantate candra karajā-
sādadhikakālaprakāśakatvena vyatirekāladdhāro vyaṅgyaḥ ||
“daśānanakirīṭebhyastatvaṇaṃ rājamaśriyaḥ |
maṇivyājena paryyastāḥ pṛthivyāmaśruvindavaḥ” |
catra kaviprauḍhoktisiddhenāpaśutyasakāraṇa bhaviṣyadrācamaśrī-
vināśarūpaṃ vastu vyajyate ||
“dhantile navamaṇikāsamudayo iskhe sitāmbhoruhaṃ
hāra: kaṣṭhataṭe payodharayuge śrīkhaṇḍalepo ghanaḥ |
eko'pi cikaliṅgabhūmitilaka khatkīrttirāśiyayā
mānāmaṇḍanatāṃ purandarapurīvāmabhuṣāṃ vigrahe" ||
atra kaviprauḍhoktisiḍrena rūpakālaṅkāreṇa “bhūmiṣṭho'pi
svargasthānāmupakāraṃ karoṣoti” vibhāvanālaṅkāro vyajyate ||
“śikhariṇi ka nu nāma kibacciraṃ kimabhidhānamasākkaro tapaḥ |
sumukhi yena tavādharapāṭalaṃ daśati vimbaphalaṃ śukaśāvakaḥ” ||
atrānena kavinibaṅkasya kasyacit kāminaḥ prauḍhoktisiddhena
bastumā tavādharaḥ puṣyātiśayalabhya iti vastu pratīyate |
"subhage koṭisālamupetya madanāumaiḥ |
vasante pañcatā tyaktā pañcatāsīdiyogināṃ” ||
atra kavinibaddhava prauḍhokimiḍūna kāmaśarāṇāṃ koṭi-
prāpyā nikhilaviyogimaraṇena vastunā bharāṇāṃ pañcatā
sāhityadarpaye|
śarān vimucya viyominaḥ śritetyutprecāsakāro vyajyate ||
"matikāmukule pachi bhāti muñcanmadhutrataḥ
prayāne pañcavālasya śaṅkhamāpūravanniva” ||
atra kavinibaddhava prauḍhoktisiddhena utprekṣāladvāreha kāma-
syāyamuśmādakaḥ kālaḥ prāptasatkathaṃ “mānini māgaṃ na muñcamīti”
vastu banyate ||
“mahilāsahassabharie tuha hima suhama vā śramātrantī |
anudiṇamaṇanammāṅgaṃ anupi tamu e ” * ||
“atrāmāśrantīti” kaviniṣaddhava prauḍholisiddhena kāvya-

viśeṣeotyalaṅkāro vyajyate | na khalu kaveḥ kavimibaddhayeva rāgā-
yāviṣṭatā| ataḥ kavinivaḍūbakraprauḍoktiḥ kaviproṭophera dhika
sahṛdaya camatkāriṇīti pṛthakpratipāditā | eṣu ca trakhakṛti-
vyaścamasthale rūpeotprekṣaṇa vyatirecanādimātrasya prādhānyaṃ saha-
dayasaṃvedyaṃ na tu rūpyādīnāmityala tereva mukhyatvaṃ ||

mayā nāṭakamupādīyata iti kaverabhiprāyaḥ sūcita iti” |
(383) pūrvva vidhāyaiva sūtradhāro nivarttate |
praviśya sthāpakastaḍata kāvyamākhthāpayettataḥ ||
divyamartyeṣu tapo miśramanyatarastayoḥ |
svacayedvastu vījaṃ vā mukhaṃ pātramathāpi vā ||
kāvyasyārthasya sthāpanāt sthāpakaḥ | tadaditi sūtradhārasa-
dṛtraguṇākāraḥ | idānīṃ pūrvvaraṅgasya samyakprayogābhāvādeka
eva sūtradhāraḥ savvaṃ prayojayatīti vyavahāraḥ | sa lāpako divyaṃ
vastu diyo bhulā ma marce bhūlā mitraca divyamartyayoranya-
taro bhulā khacayet | vaslitivṛtaṃ yathodātarāghave ||
"rāmo mūrhi vidhāya kānanamagānmālāmivājāṃ gurom-
tagatvā bharatena rājyamakhilaṃ mātrā marevojjhitaṃ |
sāhityadarpaye|
to sugrīvavibhīṣabhāvanumatī jotī para sampada
protsitā daśakandharaprabhṛtayo dhvastāḥ samastā diṣaḥ ||”
vījaṃ yathā ratnāvasyāṃ |
“dīpādavyamādapi madhyādapi jalanidherdiśo'pyantāt |
mānīva jhaṭiti ghaṭayati vidhirabhimatamabhimukhībhūtaḥ ||”
atra hi samudra vahijabhaṅgamannotthitāyā ratnāvasyā anukū-

ratnāvalīprāptau vojaṃ | mukhaṃ śeṣādinā prastutavṛttāntapratipā-
dako vāgviśeṣaḥ |
yathā |
“śrāsāditaprakaṭanila candrahāsaḥ
prāptaḥ śaratsamaya eṣa viślaṅkakāntaḥ |
utkhāya gāḍhatamasaṃ ghanakālamugraṃ
rāmo daśāsyamiva sambhṛtabandhujīvaḥ ||”
pātraṃ yathā śākunta le |
"tavāsmi gītirāgela hāriyā pramabhaṃ itaḥ |
eṣa rājeva duṣyantaḥ sāraṅgeyātiraṃhasā ||”

sūtradhāraṃ bhavedbhāva iti vai pāripārśvikaḥ |
sūcadhāro māriṣeti caṇḍe ityadhamaiḥ samāḥ ||
vayasyetyuttamaico madhyairārkheti cāgrajaḥ |
bhagavanviti vaktavyāḥ sarvvedevarṣi sinaḥ ||
sāhitvadaye|
vadedrācīca ceṭīca bhavatīti vidūṣakaḥ |
āyuvāvathinaṃ to vṛddhaṃ tāteti cetaraḥ ||
vatsa putraka tāteti nāmnā gotreṇa vā sutaḥ |
śiṣyo'nujaya vaktavyo'mātya ārmeti cādhamaiḥ ||
viprairayamamātyeti saciveti ca bhaṇyate |

agṛhītābhidhaḥ pūjyaḥ śiṣyādyairvinigadyate |
upādhyāyetiṃ cācāryyā mahārājeti bhūpatiḥ ||
svāmoti yuvarājastu kumāro bhartadārakaḥ |
saumya bhadramukhetyevamadhamaistu kumārakaḥ |
vācyā prakṛtiṃbhīrājñaḥ kumārī bhartṛdārikā ||
patiryathā tathā vācyā jyeṣṭhamadhyādhamaistriyaḥ |
ileti sadṛśī preṣyā iñje veśyā'ñjakā tathā ||

atha bhāṣāvibhāgaḥ |

atra "mitā bhūḥ patyāpāṃ saca patirapāmiti” yuktaḥ
pāṭha: | evaṃ |
"yaśo'dhigantuṃ sukhaliyā vā
manuṣyasaṃkhyāmativarttituṃ vā |
nirutsukānāmabhiyogabhājāṃ
samutsukevāddhamupaiti siddhiḥ" ||
atra sukhamohitumityucitaṃ | atrādyayāḥ prakṛtiviṣayaḥ prakra-
mabhedaḥ |ḍhatīce paryāyaviṣayaḥ | caturthe pratyayaviṣayaḥ | evamanyat |
saptamaparichedaḥ |

“macīrādiṣu raṇitaprāyaṃ paciṣu tu kūjitaprabhṛtiḥ |
stanitamaṇitādi surate meghādiṣu garjitapramukhamityādi "||
khānakha padatā yathā |
"tīrthe tadīye gajasetubandhāt pratīpaṇāmuttarato'ya gaṅgāṃ
ayanavāyavyajanībabhūvui~mā nabholaṅganaslolapacāḥ” ||
atra tadīcapadāt pūrvvaṃ gaṅgāmityaskha pāṭho yuktaḥ | evaṃ
“hitān na ya: saṃśṭaṇute sa kiṃ prabhuḥ” | atra saṃghaṭaṇuta ityataḥ
pūva~ mañaḥ sthitirucitā | atra ca "padamātrasyāsyāne niveśe'pi
samvameva vākyaṃ vivacitārthapratyāyane mantharamiti” vākyadoṣatā|
evamanyatrāpi | iha ke'pyāja : “padaśabdena vācaka eva prayogo
nigaccate na ca maño vācakatā nirvivādāt svātacceṇārthabodhana-
virahāditi” yathā “iyaṃ gataṃ ityādī " tvamityanantaraṃ cakā-
rānupādānādakramatā tathānāpīti | askhānastha samāsatā yathā |

khātuṃ vāñchati māna eva dhigiti krodhādivālohitaḥ |
proccaddūrataraprasāritakaraḥ karṣa tyaso tatvayāt
phulatkairavakoṣaniḥsaradaliśreṇīkṛpāṇaṃ śaśī” ||
aba kopina utī samāso na kṛtaḥ kaveru kṛtaḥ | vākyā-
ntarapadānāṃ vākyāntare'nupraveśa: solalaṃ | yathā |

sāhityadarpaye|

praveśo garbhitatā | yathā |
“ramaṇe caraṇaprānte pratipravaṇe'dhunā |
vadāmi sakhi te tavaṃ kadācikācitāḥ krudhaḥ” ||
arthadoṣānā |
(57) apuṣṭaduṣkamagrāmyavyāhatājhīlakaṣṭatāḥ |
anavīkṛta nirhetuprakāśitaviruddhatāḥ ||
sandigdhapunaruktatve khyātividyāviruddhate |

sadā dhante bhuvaṃ preṣaḥ sadā dhīro'vikatmanaḥ” ||
atra sadatyanavīkṛtaṃ prabhāsa padaskha parthābāntarekheopādāne
sāhityadarpaye |
'pi yadi nānyadicchityantaraṃ tadāsya doṣasya sadbhāva iti

bibhati śeṣaḥ satataṃ dharitrīṃ ṣaṣṭhāṃśavṛtterapi dharma eṣa: " ||
“gṭahītaṃ yenāsīḥ paribhavabhayānocitamapi

atra dvitīyaśastramocane hetunaka iti nirhetutvaṃ |

"acalā abalā vā syuḥ mevyā vrata manīṣiṇaḥ” ||

atra dvitīyārddhavyatirekeṇa ditīyapādasyaivārtha iti puna -
rukatā | prasiddhiviruddhatā yathā |
"tatacacāra samare śitaladharī hari : ” |
aba hare: śūlaṃ loke'prasiddhaṃ | yathā vā |
“pādāghātādaśokaste sañcātāṅkurakasṭakaḥ 1

atra strīratnamupecitumityākāṅkṣati |

atra sajjanaḥ kāminī ca śobhanā tatmahacara: khalo'śo-

cana na rāvaṇa ityetāvataiva samāpyaṃ |

atra ratnānāṃ nidherityaviśeṣa eva vācyaḥ |
"āvartta eva nābhiste netre nīlasaroruhe |

yāvartta eveti niyamo na vācyaḥ |
“yānti mīlanicolinyo rajanīvvabhisārikāḥ” |
atra tamilāsthiti rajanīviśeṣo vācyaḥ |
sāhityadarpaye|
“śrāpātasurase bhoge nimagrāḥ kiṃ na kurvvate" |
aca āpāta eveti niyamo vācyaḥ | manu vāccasyānabhi
dhāne "vyatikramalavam” ityādāvaperabhāva: dū caivakārasyeti
ko'nayorbhedaḥ | atrāha| niyamasyāvacanameva pṛthagbhūtaṃ niyama-

tāyāṃ niyāmakābhāvāt | tat kā gatiriti cet "vyatikrama-
lavamityādī” śabdocāraṇānantarameva doṣapratibhāsaḥ dūra
tvarthapratyayānantaramiti bhedaḥ | evañca śabdaparivṛttisa itvāsaha-
tyābhyāṃ pūrvairāto'pi śabdārthadoṣavibhāga evaṃ parthyavasvati |
yo doṣaḥ śabdaparivṛtyamahaḥ ma śabdadoṣa eva | yaca padārthā
nvayapratītipūrvvabodhyaḥ so'pi śabdadoṣaḥ | bañcārthapratītyanantara-
bodhyaḥ so'rthaśrapa iti | evaṃ cāniyamaparivṛttatvāderapyadhi-
kapadatlāderbhedo boddhavyaḥ | śramataparārthaye tu “rāmamanmatha-
bharaṇetyādī” niyamena vākyavyāpitvābhiprāvādākyadoṣatā |

"ānanditamapakṣo'mā parapacān inivyati” |
tra parapakṣaṃ hatvā svapañcamānandacivyatīti vidheyaṃ |

birahiprāṇaharaca kadarthava na māṃ vṛthā” ||
atra virahiṇa kī hatīyapārakhārthe nānuvādyaḥ |
"lagnaṃ rāgāvṛtācyā sudṛḍhamiha yayaivāsi baccārikaṇḍe

saptamaparichedaḥ |
tatsako'yaṃ na kiñcidraṇayati viditaṃ te'stu tenāstri dattā

cana viditaṃ te'slityanena samāpitamapi vacanaṃ tenetyādi
punarupāntaṃ || atha rakhadoṣānāha |
(577) rasasyoktiḥ svaśabdena sthāyisañcāriṇorapi |

atra ra sasyoddīpamālambanavibhāvāvamubhāvaparyavasāyinī svi

iti vata viṣamā daśāsya deī
paribhavati prasabhaṃ kimana kurmā:” ||
atra ratiparihārādīnāṃ karuṇādāvapi sambhavāt kāmi-

mañjaya rājanāyikayoḥ svayaṃ kṛtaṃ vasantasya varṇanamanādṛtya

badhaḥ | yathā vā kumārasambhave | uttamadevatayāḥ pātīparame

(578) ebhyaḥ pṛthagalaṅkāradoṣāṇāṃ naiva sambhavaḥ |
ebhyaḥ ukradoṣebhyaḥ | tathā hi upamāyāmasādṛśyāsambhavayo-

hara usahara ityādī” | upamāyāñca sādhāraṇa dharmasthādhi-

khadyoto'pi na kampate pracalituṃ madhye'pi tejasvināṃ

ukradoṣāṣāñca kacidadoṣatvaṃ kacidruṣatvamityāha |
(578) vaktari krodhasaṃyukta tathā vācce samudrate |
raudrādau tu rase'tyantaṃ duḥśravatvaṃ guṇo bhavet ||
eṣu cāsvādasvarūpaviśeṣātmaka tathā mukhyaguṇaprakarṣopakāri
lāhuṣa iti vyapadeśo bhātaḥ | krameṇa yathā |
“tadicchedalaśasya kaṣṭhaluṭhitaprāṇasya me niyaṃ
krūraḥ pañcaśaraḥ śarairatiśitairbhindan mano nirbharaṃ |

“unmaṣvavjalakuñcarendrarabhasāsphālānubandhoddhataḥ
sarvvīḥ parvvatakandarodarabhuvaḥ kurvyam pratidhvāminīḥ |
uccairuccarati dhvaniḥ śrutipathāmāthī yathā'yantathā
prāyaḥpreṅkhadamayaśaṅkhadhavaslā veleya mudracchati” |
ayaṃ bandhavaikavyātmakatvādoja eva | samādhirārohāvaroha-
kamarūpaḥ | āroha utkarṣaḥ | avaroho'pakarṣastayoḥ kramo vaira-
sthānāvako vinyāsaḥ || yathā “cañcagujetyādi” | atra pādatraye
krameṇa bandhasya gāḍhatā | caturthapāde lapakarṣaḥ | tasyāpi tīvra-
sāhibadarpaye |
pravamocārgyatathā projasvitā | udāratā vikaṭalalacacā |
vikaṭalañca padānāṃ nṛtyatprāyatvaṃ | vathā |
“mācaraca viniviṭernapurairnartakīnāṃ
jhacitiraṇitamāsīttatra citraṃ kalañca” |
atra ca tanmatānusāreṇa rasāmusandhānamantareṇaiva śabdaprauḍhā-
krimāneṣajaḥ | prasāda obomiśrita maithisyātmā | yathā |
“yo yaḥ śastaṃ bibhartti svabhujagurumadaḥ pāṇḍavīnāṃ camūnāṃ
yo vaḥ pañcālagoce triradhikavayā garbhaśayyāṃ gato vā |
yo castatkarmasācī carati mavi raṇe yaca yaca pratīpaḥ
krodhānbhalava tatra svayamiha jagatāmantakasyānta ko'haṃ " |

bathā| “śvāsānmuñcatītyādi” |

((30) kacittu kāyācityādanyathā racanādayaḥ |
sāhibadabe |

tyādī” || prabandhaucityācathā nāṭakādau | raudre'ṣabhinaya-

re'pi na masṛṣavarṣādayaḥ | kathāyāṃ raudre'pi nātyantamuddhatāḥ |
evamanyadapi jñeyaṃ ||

udāharaṇaṃ |

(649) anyasyānyārthakaṃ vākyamanyathā yojayedyadi |

yathā mama |

“kiraṇā haribāila daciṇaca samīraṇaḥ |
kāntotsaṅgajuṣāṃ nūnaṃ sarvva eva sudhākiraḥ” ||
atra "sudhākira iti” cipkapratyayavāḥ | kiñcātra bahu-
vacanaikava canayoreka rūpyādacanaceṣo'pi |
"vikasako jamīlākhe tathā tayāḥ khanadathī |
tava datāṃ sadā modaṃ lasantaralahāriṇī” ||
atra napuṃsakastrīliṅgayoḥ dveṣo vacanadveṣo'pi |
“ayaṃ sarvāṇi śāstrāṇi hṛdi śeṣu ca baccati |
sāmarthyahadamitrācāṃ mitrāṣāñca nṛpātmajaḥ” ||
ana bacyatīti vahivacāḥ sāmarthakaditi chantatikarotyoḥ
pratyoḥ |
"pṛthu kārttakharapātramityādi” |
atra padabhaṅge vibhaktisamāsavorapi vailacacāt padoṣo na
tu prakṛtiśeṣaḥ | evañca |
"nītānā mākulībhāvaṃ sumberiśilīmukheḥ |
sāhityadarpaye |
sadṛśe vanavṛddhānāṃ kamalānāṃ tadīcale” ||
atra sudhaśilīmukhādiśabdānāṃ triṣṭatve'pi vibhanerabhedāt
prakṛtiśeṣa: | anyathā marvvaca padaśeṣaprasaṅgaḥ |
"sarvvasvaṃ hara sarvvasya tvaṃ bhavachedatatparaḥ |
nayopakāramāṃmukhyamāyāsi tanuvarttanaṃ " |
aba hara iti pakṣe śivasambodhanamiti sup | pakṣe ca hadhā-
tostiṅiti vibhakteḥ | evaṃ bhavetyādī asya ca bhedasya pratyaya-
leṣeṇāpi gatārthale pratyayāntarāmādhyasubantatiṅantagatatvena vi-
chittiviśeṣāśrayaṇāt pṛthagutiḥ |
"mahade surasandhame tamava samāsaṅgamāgamāharaṇe |
hara bahusaraṇaṃ taṃ cittamohamavasara ume sahamā" ||
atra| saṃskṛtamahārācyoḥ |
(644) punastridhā sabhaṅgo'thābhaṅgastadubhayātmakaḥ ||
etadbhedatrayañca uktabhedāṣṭake yathā sambhavaṃ jñeyaṃ | yathā vā |
“yena dhvastamanobhavena balijitkāyaḥ purā svīkṛto
yacāhṛntabhujaṅgahāravalayo gaṅgāñca yo'dhārayat|
yasyāḥ śaśimacchiro hara iti stutyañca nāmāmarāḥ
pāvāt sa svayamandhakacayakarasvāṃ sarvvadeomādhavaḥ” ||
atra "yena dhvastetyādī" sabhaṅgameṣa: | “andhaketyādī”
abhaṅgaḥ | anayosaikatra sambhavāt sabhaṅgābhaṅgātmako granthagaurava-
bhayāt pṛthaṅgodāhṛtaḥ | iha kecidāja: "sabhaṅgadveṣa eva śabda-
daśamaḥ parichedaḥ |
śeṣaviṣayaḥ | yatrodāttādisvarabhedādbhinna prayatvocāryyatvena bhi-
nayeāḥ śabdayorjjatukāṣṭhanyāyena leṣa: | abhaṅgastu arthaśeṣa eva |
yatra kharābhedādabhinna prayatnācārgyatayā śabdābhedādarthayoreka-
tṛmtagataphaladayanyāyena hyeṣa:, yo hi yadāśritaḥ sa tadalaṅkāra
eva, alaṅkārthyāladdharaṇabhāvasya lokavadāśrayāyibhāvenopapa-
tiriti” tadanye ma manyante, tathā atra dhvaniguṇībhūtavyajyado-
bagulālaṅkārāṇāṃ śabdārthagatalena vyavasmiteranvayavyatirekāmu
vidhāyilena niyama iti | na ca "andhakacayetyā do" śabdā-
bhedaḥ | “arthabhedena śabdabhed” iti darśanāt | kiñcātra śabdasyaiva
mukhyatayā vaicitryabodhopāyalena kavipratibhayohaṇāt śabdā-
laṅkārameva | visadṛśabdadayasya bandhe caivaṃvidhasya vaicitrya-
sthābhāvād vaicitryasyaiva cālaṅkāravādarthamukha precitayā cārthī-
lahāratve'nuprāsādīnāmapi rakhādiparatvemārthamukha precitayārthī-
laṅkāratvaprasaṅgaḥ | śabdasyābhitrapravato cālenāthīlaṅkārale
“pratikūlatāmupagate hi vidhāvityādī” śabdabhede'pyarthīlaṅkā-
ratvaṃ tavāpi pramajyatītyubhayatrāpi śabdālakāratvameva | yatra tu
śabda parivarttane'pi na śeṣalakhaṇḍamā tatra |
'sokemonnatimāyāti tokenāyātyadhogatiṃ |
ho sumadṛśī vṛttistulākoṭaḥ khalasya ca” ||
ityādāvarthaśleṣaḥ | “asya cālaṅkārāntaravivikraviṣayatāyā
asambhavāt vidyamānevbalaṅkārāntarevyapavādalena tadādhakatayā
tatpratibho tyattihetutvamiti” kecit | ityamatra vicārgyate
sāhityadarpaye |
samāmā prastutapraśaṃmādau dvitovārthasthānabhidheyatayā nā
gandho'pi “vidanmānasa haṃsetyāī ” sevagarbhe rūpake'pi mānasa-
śabdasya cittamarārūpobhayārthatve'pi rūpakeṇa śreṣo vādhyate |
barorūpasyaivārthasya viśrāmadhāmatayā prādhānyāt | śreṣe arthadaya-
sthāpi camakañcatvaṃ “sannihitabālāndhakārā bhāsvambhūtiyetyā-
do" virodhābhāve'pi viruddhārthasya pratibhātta mātrasya prarohā-
bhāvāna dveṣaḥ | evaṃ punarukravadābhāme'pi | tena " yena dhvotyā-
do” prākāra śikayornottānāmityādāvaprākaraṇikayo re kadhī-
bhisambandhāt tusyayogitāyāṃ |
"secchopajātaviṣayo'pi na yāti vaktuṃ
dehīti mārgaṇataica dadāti duḥkhaṃ |
mohāta samutkṣipati jīvanamadhyakāṇḍe
kaṣṭaṃ prasnanaviśisyaḥ prabhuraspabuddhiḥ ||”
isya prākaraṇikāprākaraṇikayāre kadharmābhisambandhāddīpake |
"sakasakalaṃ purametajjātaṃ samprati sudhāṃśuvimvamityāda”
copamāyāṃ vidyamānāyāmapi śeṣaskhetadviṣayaparihāreṇāsā-
vāt | eṣāñca śreṣaviṣayaparihāreṇāpi sthiteḥ | etadviṣaye levastha
prābalyena camatkāritvapratīteca śreṣeṇaiva vyapadeśo bhavituṃ yuktaḥ,
anyathā tāpadeśasya sarvvathā'bhāvaprasaṅgāceti | atrocyate na
tāvat paramārthataḥ jeṣasthālaṅkārāntarāvivikraviṣayatā | “yena
dhvasetyādinā” vivikraviṣayatvāt, na cātra tujhyayogitā, tastrāca
dvayorapyarthayorbsa ccatvaniyamābhāvāt | atra hi mādhavomādhava-
daśamaḥ parivedaḥ |
vorakasya vācyatvaniyame'parasya vyaṅgyatvaṃ syāt | kiñca tulyayo-
gitāvāmekasyaiva dharmasthānekadharmasambaddhatayā pratītiḥ ihalane-
keṣāṃ dharmiṇāṃ pṛthak pṛthak dharmasambadbhuta thā| “sakalakale tyādī"
moṣamāpratibhotpattihetuḥ śeṣaḥ | pūrvopamāyā nirviṣayatvā
pateḥ | “kamalamiva mukhaṃ manojametadityāyasti ghūpamāvi
ṣaya iti cenna | yadi "sakale tyādī" śabdaleṣatayā mopamā
tat kimaparāddhaṃ “manojñamityādāvarthaśeṣeṇa sphuṭamadhīlaṅkā-
rāvetāvupamāsamucayo, kintu “trāśritya śabdamācaṃ sāmānyami-
hāpi sambhavataḥ” iti rudraṭodiśā guṇakriyāmāmyavacchanda-
bāmbasvāṣṇupamāprayojakatvāt | nanu guṇakriyā sāmyasyaiva upamā
prayojakatā yuktā tatra sādhamaryasya vāstavalāt, śabdasāmyasya tu na
tathā tatra sādhamaryasyāvāstavatvāt, tataśca pūrlepamāyā anyathā
nupapaterguṇa kiyā sāmyasyaivārthaśreṣaviṣayatāparityāme pūrṇeśapamā-
viṣayatā yuktā na tu "saka khetyā do” bhabdasāmbasyeti cena |
sādharmyamupametyevāviśiṣṭasya upamālacalasya śabdasānyāyāṭa -
orabhāvām | yadi ca śabdasāmye sādhamaryamavāstavatvānnopamā-
prayojakaṃ tadā kathaṃ “viinmāmasetyādī” ādhārabhūte cintā-
do sarovarāyāropo rājādeu~sāyāroparūparūpakaḥ prayojakaḥ |
kiñca yadi vāstavasāmya evopamāṅgīkārya tadā kathaṃ tvayāpi
“sakalakaletyādī” vādhyabhūtāpamāṅgīkriyate | kiñcātra śeṣasyaika
sāmyanibāhakatā na tu sāmyasya śeṣanirvvāhakatā, śeṣabandhataḥ
prathamaṃ sāmyasyāsambhavādityupamāyā evāṅgilena vyapadeśo jyā-
sāhityadarpaye|
thān | “pradhānena vyapadeśā bhavantī" tyutanayāt | nanu śabdāla-
dvāraviṣaye'ṅgāṅgibhāvasaharī nāṅgīkivate tat kathamaca śeṣo
pamacoraṅgāṅgibhāvaḥ bahara iti cena| arthānusandhānavirahi-
sanuprāsādāveda tathānaṅgīkārāt | evaṃ dīpakādāvapi jñeyaṃ |
“satpacā madhuragiraḥ prasādhitāśā madoṅtārabhāḥ |
nipatanti dhārmarār̤āḥ kālavaśā medinīpṛṣṭe” ||
śrama śaradarbanayā prakaraṇena dhārntarāṭrādiśabdānāṃ iṃsādya-
thābhidhāne niyamāt duryodhanādirūpo'rthaḥ śabdaśatimo va
studhvaniḥ | iha ca prakṛtaprabandhābhidheyakha ditīvakhārthasya saca-
tathaiva vivacitalā dupamānopameyabhāvo na vivacita iti nopa-
mādhvamirma śeṣa iti sarvvamavadātaṃ |
(645) padmādyākāra cetutve varṇanāṃ citramucyate |
ādiśabdāt khadmurajacakragomūtrikādayaḥ | asya ca tathā
vidhakhipisa
triveśaviśeṣavaśena camatkāra vidhāvināmapi varṣānāṃ
tathāvidhaśrocā
kāśasamavāyaviśeṣavaśena camatkāravidhāvibhi-
barserabhedenopacārācchabdālaṅkāratvaṃ | tatra padmabandho vathā mama |
"māramāsuṣamā cārurucā māravadhūtamā |
māntadhūrttatamāvāsā sā vāmā me'stu mā ramā” ||
eṣo'ṣṭadasvapadmabandho digdaleṣu nirgamapraveśābhyāṃ triṣṭavarcaḥ
kintu vidigdale vanyathā kartikācarantu siṣṭametra evaṃ khar̤a-
bandhādikamapyūyaṃ |
daśamaḥ parichedaḥ |
(646) kāvyāntarganubhūtā yā sā tu neca prapaccāle |
rasasya paripanthitvānnālaṅkāraḥ prahelikā |
uktivaicityamātrā sā cyutadattātarādikā |
cutācarā dattācarā cutadantākṣarā ca | udāharaṇaṃ |
"kūjanti kokilā: sāle yovane phulamambujaṃ |
kiṃ karotu kuraṅgālī vadanena mipīḍitā” ||
atra “rasākha iti” vaktavye “sāla iti" racyutaḥ | “baje”
ityaca “yovana" iti yātaḥ | " vadanenetyaja" "madaneneti”
masyuto vo dantaḥ | ādiśabdāt kriyākārakagupyādayaḥ | tatra
kiyāguptirthayā |
"pāṇḍavānāṃ sabhāmadhye duryodhama upāgataḥ |
tasmai gāca hiravañca sabasyābharaṇāni ca” ||
atra “duryodhana” ityatra "traduryo'dhana iti” aduriti
kriyā guptā | evamanyatrāpi | athāvasaraprāpteṣvarthāsahāreṣu prā-
dhānyāt sādṛśyamūleṣu lacitavyeṣu teṣāmapyupajīvyatvena prathamamu-
pamālaṅkāramā |
(647) sāmyaṃ vācyamavaidhayeṃ vākako upamādayoḥ |
rūpakādiṣu sāmyasya vyaṅgatvaṃ vyatireke ca vaidhamaryasyāpyutiḥ
upameyopamāyāṃ vākyahavaṃ ananvaye ca ekasyaiva sāmyophiri-
tyasyābhedaḥ |
sāhityadarpaye |
(648) sā pūrṇa yadi sāmānyadharmā aupamyavāci ca
upameyaṃ copamānaṃ bhaveddāccaṃ
sā upamā sādhāraṇadhī dayoḥ sādṛśyahetugula kriye
manojñatvādi, aupamyavācakamivādi, upameyaṃ mukhādi, upa-
mānaṃ candrādi |
iyaṃ punaḥ |
śrautī yatheṣavāśabdā ivārthī yā vaniryadi ||
ārtho tulyasamānādyāstulyārthī yatra vā batiḥ |
yathevavādayaḥ śabdā upamānāntara prayukta tuyādipada sādhā-
raṇā api śrutimātreṇopamānopamevagatamādṛṣyalacaṇasambandhaṃ
bodhayantīti tatsadbhāve śrota upamā | evaṃ tatra "tasyaivetyanena”
vārthe vihitasya vartarupādāne | tulyādayastu "kamalena tujhyaṃ
mukhamityādī” upameya eva | kamadaṃ mukhasya tulyamityādāvupa-
māna eva | kamalaṃ mukhañca tulyamityādāvubhayatrāpi viśrāmyantī-
ti arthīnusandhānādeva sāmyaṃ pratipādayantīti tatsadbhāve prārthī |
evaṃ "tena tuyamityādinā" tulyārthe vihitasya varupādāne |
(648) he tadvite samāse'tha vākye
he tīrtho | udāharaṇaṃ |
"maurabhamanbhoruhavanmuskhasya kumbhāvita stano pīnī |
hṛdayaṃ madayati vadanaṃ tava śaradinduryathā bāle” ||
daśamaḥ parichedaḥ |
atra krameṇa vividhā śrītī |
"madhuraḥ sudhāvadadharaḥ pavatulyo'tipelavaḥ pāṇi: |
cakitamṭagalā canābhyāṃ sadṛśo capaleca locane tasyāḥ " ||
atra krameṇa cividhā prārtho |
pūrṇa ṣaḍeva tat |
(651) luptā sāmānyadharmādarekasya yadi vā iyoḥ |
trayāṇāṃ vānupādāne śrautyārthī sāpi pūrvvavat ||
sā luptā | tadbhedamāha |
spaṣṭaṃ |
(652) pūrṇavaddharmmalope sā vinā śrautīntu taddhite |
sā luptopamā dharmā sādhāraṇaguṇakriyārūpasya lope pūrṇā-
vaditi pūrvvekarītyā ṣaṭprakārā, kinvatra taddhite śrotyā śrama-
bhavāt pañcamakārā | udāharaṇaṃ |
"mukhaminduryathā pāṇi: palavena samaḥ priye |
vācaḥ sudhā ivoṣṭhaste vimbatulyā mano'bhavat ” ||
(653) ādhārakarmma vidhi dvividhe ca kyaca kyaṅi |
karmmakatraurṇamuli ca syādevaṃ pañcadhā punaḥ |
dharmalāpe suptetyanuṣañyate | kyackyaṅṇamulaḥ kalāpamate yi
bāyiṇama: | krameṇodāharaṇaṃ |
“antaḥpurībasi raṇeṣu sutīyasi tvaṃ
poraṃ janaṃ tava sadā ramaṇīyate śrīḥ |
sāhityadarpaje |
dṛṣṭaḥ priyābhiramṛtayutidarśamindra-
sañcāramatra bhuvi sañcarasi citośa” ||
atrāntaḥpurīyasītyatra" sukhavihārāsadalasya "sutīya-
sītyatra” le hanirbhara tvamsa ca sādhāraṇadharmasya lopaḥ | evama-
nyatra | iha ca yathāditasyādivirahāt śrautyādiviśeṣacintā
nāsti | idañca kecit śraupamya pratipādakasya vatekhāpe udāra-
ramti tadayutaṃ | kyaṅāderapi tadarthavihitatvenaupamyapratipāda-
katvāt | nanu kyaṅādiṣu samyagaupamyapratītināsti pratyayatve-
nāsvatantratvāt ivādiprayogābhāvāceti na vācyaṃ | kalpavādā-
vapi tathā prasaṅgāt | na ca kalpavādīnāmivāditu svatayaupa-
myasya vācakatvaṃ, kyaṅādīnānsu srotakalaṃ, ivādīnāmapi vāca -
kale nicathābhāvāt | vācakatle vā samuditaṃ padaṃ vācakaṃ "prata-
tipratyayo svakhārthabodhakāviti ca" matadaye'pi vatyādikā-
ṅāyoḥ sāmyameveti | yaca kecidāja: “vatyādaya dravācyathā
anuśiṣyante kyaṅādayasvā cārārthe iti" tadapi na | na khalu
kyaṅādaya śrācāramātrārthī api tu sādṛśyācārārthī iti |
tadevaṃ dharmalope daśaprakārā luptā |
(654) upamānānupādāne dvidhā vākyasamāsayoḥ |
udāharaṇaṃ |
"tasyā mukhena saṅkaṇaṃ ramyaṃ nāste na vā mayanatulyaṃ” |
atra mukhanayanapratinidhivasvantara yogya mānatvādupamāna-
daśamaḥ parichedaḥ |
lopa: | acaiva ca "mukhena sadṛśaṃ" ityatra "mukhaṃ yathedamiti”
“nayanatulamityāca" "dṛgiveti" pāṭhe śrītyapi sambhavatītya-
nayorbhedabā: pratyekaṃ śrotyārthīlabhedena caturvidhatvasambhave'pi prā-
comarītyā ḍiprakāratva mevākaṃ |
(655) aupamyavācinā lope samāse kkiṃpi ca dvidhā |
krameṇodāharaṇaṃ |
"vadanaṃ mṛgaśāvācyā ḥ sudhākaramanohara” |
"gaībhati śrutiparuṣaṃ śakraṃ minadan mahātmanāṃ purataḥ” ||
atra "gaībhatītyana" śropamyavācina: kkipo lopa: | na
ceha upameyasyāpi slopa: | “nimadaniti” anenaiva nirdeśāt |
(656) didhā samāse bāstreca lope dharmopamānayoḥ | |
“tasyā mukhenetyādī” “ramyamiti” sthāne "loke iti”
pāṭhe 'nagromadāharaṇaṃ |
(657) ki samāsagatā dvedhā dharme vādivilopane |
udāharaṇaṃ | “vidhavati mukhābamasyāḥ" |
atra ‘‘vidhavatoti” manoharatvakkippratyayayorlepaḥ | keci-
atrāpi pratyayalopamāū: | “mukhābamiti” ca samāsagā|
((58) upameyasya lope tu syādekā pratyaye kāci
yathā |
sāhityadarpaye |
"rātivikra mākho kavikasvara trilocanaḥ |
kṛpālodagradoīschaḥ sa sahasābudhībati" |
catra sa sahalāyudhamiva cātmānamācaratīti vāko upame-
bāmano lopaḥ | na ceha aupamlavācakalopa uhādeva nyā-
cāt | atra kecidājaḥ sahalāyudhena saha "varttata iti” sa
sahalāyudhaḥ “sa davācaratīti” vākyāt sa sahalāyudhīvatīti
padasiddha viśeṣaṇa śabdānupapātlādihopa
meca lopa iti tatra
vicārasahaṃ | karttari kyaco'nuśāsamaviruddhatvāt |
(659) dharmepameyalope'nyā
vathā |
“yaśasi prasarati bhavataḥ cīrodīyanti bāgarāḥ sarvve” |
ana cīrodamivātmānamācarantītyupameca trātmā sādhā-
ramadharmaḥ śakatāca luptau |
cilope ca samāsagā |
yathā | “rājate mhagalocanā" |
catra mṛgasya locane drava cañcale locane yasā iti samāse
upamāpratipādakasādhāraṇadhapamānānāṃ lopaḥ |
(661) tenopamāyā bhedāḥ syuḥ saptaviṃśatisaṅkhyakāḥ |
pūrṇa paddhidhā suptā cekaviṃśatividheti militvā saptaviṃśa-
tiprakāropamā | eṣu ceopamābhedeṣu madhye aluptasādhārabadhaṣu
bhedeṣu viśeṣa : pratipādyate |
daśamaḥ parichedaḥ |
(662) ekarūpaḥ kvacit trāpi bhinnaḥ sādhārayo guṇaḥ |
bhine vignānuvimvatvaṃ śabdamāneca vā bhidā ||
tatra ekarūpe yathā udāhṛtaṃ | "madhuraḥ sudhāvadadhara ityā-
di” | vimbaprativimbale yathā |
“bhasāpavarjjitaisleṣāṃ śirobhiḥ śulaihīṃ |
tastāra saraghāvyāptaiḥ sa caudrapaṭakhairiva”||
catra “śatruberityasya” “saraghāvyāptairiti” dṛṣṭāntavatprati-
vimbanaṃ | śabdamātreṇa bhinale yathā |
“mera vidhāya navanaṃ vikasitamiva nīlamutpasaṃ mavi sā |
kathayāmāsa kṛśāṅgī manogataṃ nikhilamākūtaṃ " ||
atha eke eva soratvavikasitale prativastūpamāva chabdabhedena
nirdiṣṭe |

bacā|

pade pade vibhāti ma cakravākaiḥ khameriva” ||

praṇāsāhubhyaṃ gamyaṃ |
yathā |

yathorddhamupameyasya yadi syādupamānatā ||

iṃsāvate cārugatema kāmā |
kāntāvate sarvasukhena vāri
vārīcate svacchatayā vihāba: " ||
(665) mālopamā badekasyopamānaṃ bahu dṛśyate |
yathā |

((80) sandeśaḥ prakṛte'nyasya saṃśayaḥ pratibhotthitaḥ |

nāyamalaṅkāraḥ |

katvāt| kecidāṅaḥ “ayamalāro niyamenāvaśyayāvenā-

rati tatsadbhāve'pi ca grahīṭabhedena nānātva pratītirūpo vi
chittiviśeṣa ullekhāsyabhinālaṅkāraprayojakaḥ | śrīkaṇṭhajana-
padavarṣane “vañcapañcaramiti" "śaraṇāgataiḥ ambaravivaramiti"
“vātikaiḥ” ityādivātiśayokrerviviko viṣayaḥ | dūha ca

gauṇīmūlarūpakādiprayojakatvāt | yadāhuḥ śārīrakamīmāṃ-
sābhāṣyavyākhyāne śrīmadācaspatimiśrā: “api ca paraśabdaḥ

patroḥ sampratipantiḥ sa gauṣaḥ sa ca bhedapratyayapuraḥsara iti |
iha tu vātikānāṃ śrīkaṇṭhajanapadavarṣame bhrāntikṛta eva amba-
ravivaratvācāropa iti | ameva ca "tapovanamiti” munibhiḥ
“kāmāyatanamiti”
yogaḥ | .

“dhanyāḥ khalu vane vātāḥ kahāraspardhaśītalāḥ |

antaraṃ kathayiṣyanti kākalīkovidāḥ punaḥ” ||

karuṇāvimukhena mṛtyumā haratā tvāṃ vada kiṃ na me itaṃ” ||
iti ro |
(726) vyāghātaḥ sa tu kenāpi vastu yena yathā kṛtaṃ |
tenaiva cedapāyena kurute'nyastadanyathā ||
bathā| "huśā dagdhaṃ manasijamityādi”|
(757) sokaryeṇa ca kāryyastra viruddha kriyate yadi |
vyāghāta ityeva |
"hemalaṃ tiṣṭha drutamahamahobhiḥ katipayaiḥ
samānatā kānte madurasi na cāvāsabahanā|
nāyakana nāyikāyā mṛdutvaṃ sahagamanābhāva hetu ve-
mokaṃ | nāyikayā ca pratyuta sahagamane tato'pi sokaryeṇa hetu-
tabopanyastaṃ |
sāhityadarpaye|
mṛduṣaṃ me hetuḥ subhaga bhavatā gantumadhikaṃ
na hadī moḍhā badiraichatamāvāsamasamaṃ” ||
(738) paraṃ paraṃ prati yadā pūrvvapūrvvasya cetutā |
tadā kāraṇamālā syāt
bacā|
yathā |
"śrutaṃ kṛtadhiyāṃ saṅgājjāyate vinaya: śrutāt |
lokānurāgo vijayāna kiṃ lokānurāgata:" ||
tanmālādīpakaṃ punaḥ |
dharmiṇāmekadharmeṇa sambandho yadyathottaraṃ |
“tvathi saṅgarasamprāpte dhanuṣā''sāditāḥ śarāḥ |
bharerariśirastena bhūtayā tvaṃ layā catra: ” ||
atrāsādanakriyā dharmaḥ |
(730) pūrvvaṃ pūrvvaṃ prati viśeṣaṇatvena paraṃ paraṃ |
sthāpyate'pote vā cesyāttadekāvalī didhā ||
krameṇodāharaṇaṃ |
"maro vikasitāmbhojamabhoja bhaṅgamaṅgataṃ |
bhadrā yatra saṅgītāḥ saṅgītaṃ samarodayaṃ || "
daśamaḥ parichedaḥ |
na tajjalaṃ yatra sucārupaddhajaṃ
ma paṅkajaṃ tadyadalomaṣaṭpadaṃ |
na ṣaṭpado'sau na juguca yaḥ kathaṃ
na guścitaṃ taba jahāra yanmanaḥ” ||
kacidiśeyyamapi yathāntaraṃ viśeṣaṇatayā sthāpitamapohita ca
| yathā |
“vāpyo bhavanti vimalāḥ sphuṭanti kamalāni vāpīṣu |
kamaleṣu patanyalayaḥ karoti saṅgītamaliṣu paī" |
evamapohane'pi |
(731) uttarottara mutkarṣo vastunaḥ sāra uccate |
yathā |
“rājye sāraṃ vasudhā vasudhāyāmapi puraṃ pure mādhaṃ |
maudhe talpaṃ talpe varāṅganānaṅga sarvvasvaṃ ” ||
(732) yathāsaṅkhhyamanūddeśa uddiṣṭānāṃ krameṇa yat |
bathā|
"ummīlanti masserlunīdhi vahati somāñcalegāhaṇu
kīḍākānanamāviśanti valayakāṇaiḥ samuttālaya |
ityaṃ vañjula dakṣiṇānisa kuikaṇṭheṣu sāṅketika
vyāhārāḥ subhaga ladīyavirahe tasyāḥ sakhīnāṃ mithaḥ ||”
(733) kacidekamanekasi caneka caikagaṃ kramāt |
bhavati kriyate vā cettadā paryyīya iṣyate ||
sāhityadarpaye |
krameṇa yathā |
"gvitāḥ vayaṃ pāsu tāḍitāṃdharāḥ
payodharodhanipāta cūrṇitāḥ |
valīṣu tasyāḥ skhalitāḥ pravedire
krameṇa nābhiṃ prathamodavindavaḥ |
vicaranti vilāsinyo yatra śroṇibharākhamāḥ |
hakakākaśivāstaca dhāvandharipure tava ||
vistṛṭarāgādadharātrivarttita
stanāṅgarāgādaruṇāca kandukāt |
kumākurādarājaparicatākuliḥ
kṛto'cacapraṇayī tayā karaḥ ||
yathorāropitastāro hārase'rivadhūjanaiḥ |
midhīyante tayoḥ sthūlāḥ stanayoraśruvindavaḥ ||”
eṣu ca kacidādhāraḥ saṃhatarūpo'maṃitarūpaca kacidādheya-
mapi | yathā “sthitāḥ upamityatra" udavindavaḥ paccādāvasaṃ-
itarūpa ādhāra krameṇābhavan | “vicaranti” ityanādheyabhūtā
vṛkādayaḥ saṃhatarūpāripure krameṇābhavam | evamanyat | atra
caikasthānakā krameṇaiva hatterviśeṣālaṅkārāḥ | vinimayā-
bhāvāt parivṛteḥ |
(734) paravṛttirvinimayaḥ samanyūnādhikairbhavet |
krameṇodāharaṇaṃ |
daśamaḥ parichedaḥ |
"dayā kaṭācameṇācī jagrāha hṛdayaṃ mama |
mayā tu hṛdayaṃ datlā gṛhīto madagabbaraḥ ||”
atra prathame'rddhe sameja ditīye'rje nyūnena |
“tasya ca pravayamo jaṭāyuṣaḥ svarmiṇaḥ kimiva jo pyate'dhunā
yena jarjarakalevara vyayāt krītamindukiraṇojjvayaṃ yaśaḥ ||"
atrādhikyena |
(735) praśnādato vāpi kathitādastuno bhavet |
sāhaganyavyapocaśvecchābda ārtho'thavā tadā ||
parisaṃkhyā
krameṇodāharaṇaṃ |
“kiṃ bhūṣaṇaṃ sudṛḍhamatra yo na ratnaṃ
kiṃ kārgyamārgyacaritaṃ sukṛtaṃ na doṣaḥ |
kiñcapurapratihataṃ dhiṣaṇā na netraṃ
jānāti kaskhadparaḥ sadasadvivekaṃ ||"
atra vyavacchecaṃ rajādi śābdaṃ |
“kimārāvaṃ sadā puṣyaṃ kaca sevyaḥ sadāgamaḥ |
ko dhyeyo bhagavān viṣṇuḥ kiṃ kāyaṃ paramaṃ padaṃ ||"
atra vyavacchedyaṃ pāpācārthaṃ| dhanayoḥ praśnapūrvvakalaṃ | apraśna-
pūrvvakale yathā |
“bharbhive na vibhave vyasanaṃ śāstre na yuvatikāmāstre |
cintā yaśasi na vapuṣi prāthaḥ paridṛṣyate mahatāṃ ||”
sāhityadarpaye |
atrāniṣṭatvādgamanasya vidhiḥ prassacadrūpā niṣedhe parthamasati |
viśeṣaya gamanakhātyanta parihāryatvarūpaḥ pratīyate |
(716) vibhāvanā vinā hetuṃ kāryyātpattiryaducyate |
uktānuktanimittatvād dvidhā sā parikīrttitā ||
vinā kāraṇamupanibadhyamānā'pi kāryodayaḥ kiñcidamyat
kāraṇamapekṣyaiva bhavituṃ yuktaḥ | taca kāraṇāntaraṃ kacidukaṃ kaci
danukramiti dvidhā | yathā |
“amāvāsakṛśaṃ madhyamaśahatarale ho |
abhūṣaṇamanohāri vapurvayasi subhuvaḥ” ||
ṛṇa vayārūpaṃ nimitamu | ajeṣa "vapubhati mṛgodṛśa"
iti pāṭhe'bhukaṃ |
(717) sati kṣetau phalābhāvo viśeṣoktistathā dvidhā |
tathetyuktānukranimittatvāt| tatrokranimittā yathā |
“dhamino'pi nirunmādā yuvāno'pi na cañcalāḥ |
prabhavo'pyapramatāste mahāmahimaśālinaḥ" ||
atra mahāmahimaśālitvaṃ nimittamu | atraiva caturthapāde
“kiyantaḥ santi bhūtale” iti pāṭhe tvamukaṃ | acinyanimitta
cānukranimittatvasyaiva bheda iti pṛthaṅgokaṃ | yathā |
“ma ekastrīṇi jayati jaganti kusumāyudhaḥ |
haratāpi tanuṃ yasya śambhunā va itaṃ vasaṃ" ||
daśamaḥ parichedaḥ |
catra tamuharaye'pi balā haraṇe nimitamacintyaṃ | iha cakā-
yībhāvaḥ kāryyaviruddhasabhāṣa mukhenāpi nibadhyate | vibhāvanāyā-
mapi kāraṇābhāvaḥ kāraṇaviruddhasaddhādhamukhenāpi | evañca "caḥ
kaumāraharaḥ sa eva hi vara" ityāderatkaṣṭhākāraṇaviruddhaka
nibandhanād vibhāvanā | “ya: kaumārahara" ityādī kāraṇa
kārgyaviruddhācā utkaṣṭhāvā nibandhanāviśeṣotiḥ | eva-
cāca vibhāvanāviśeṣoktyoḥ saṅkaraḥ | jhuddhodāharaṇantu mugyaṃ |
(718) jātiścaturbhijātyādyairguṇa guṇādibhistribhiḥ |
kriyā kriyādravyābhyāṃ yad dravyaṃ dravyeṇa vā mithaḥ ||
viruddhamiva bhāseta virodho'sau daśākṛtiḥ |
krameṇa yathā |
"taba virahe malayamaruḍvānala: śaśiruco'pi somāṇaḥ |
hṛdayamakhirutamapi bhinte nalinīdalamapi nidāgharavirakhāḥ ||
sajātamukha khāsaṅgākaḍatara gṛhakaghaṭanayā nṛpate |
dijapatnīnāṃ kaṭhināḥ sati bhavati karāḥ sarojasukumārāḥ ||
ajasya gṛhato janma nirīiskha itadviṣaḥ |
srūpato jāgarūkasya pāthārthaṃ veda kastava |
balabhotsaṅgasaṅgena binā hariṣacacuṣaḥ |
rākāvibhāvarījānirviṣajvālākukho'bhavat ||
nayanayugāsecanakaṃ mānasahattvāpi dupāpaṃ |
rūpamidaṃ madirācyā madayati hṛdayaṃ dunoti ca me ||
sāhityadarpaye|
"tvadājirājītyādi" "vasabhālanetyādi " jhoke caturtha-
pāde "madhyandinadinādhipa" iti pāṭhe dravyayorvirodhaḥ | ana
"taba viraha" ityādī pavanādīnāṃ baḍavyaktivācakatvā jāti-
śabdānāṃ dāvānaleoṣṇahadayabhedana sūryai jītiguṇakriyādravyarupai-
ranyonyaṃ virodho mukhata ābhāsate| virahahetukatvāt samādhānaṃ |
a "ajaskheti" śrādāvajavādiguṇasya grahaṇādikriyayā vi
rodhaḥ | bhagavataḥ prabhāvasthātiśayitvāttu samādhānaṃ | “tvadā-
botyādī” “haro'pi śirasā gaṅgāṃ na dhante” iti virodhaḥ |
“tvadājī” tyādikaviproḍhā tu samādhānaṃ | spaṣṭamanyat |
vibhāvanāyāṃ kāraṇābhāvenāpanibadhyamānatvāt kāryyameva vādhya-
leja pratīyate | viśeṣako ca kāryyābhāvena kāraṇameva | dūra
tvanyonyaṃ dayorapi vādhyatvamiti bhedaḥ |
(718) kāryyakāraṇayorbhinna deśamāyāmasaṅgatiḥ |
cathā|
"sā bālā vayamapragalbhamanasaḥ sā strī vayaṃ kātarāḥ
sā pīnokhatimatpayodharayugaṃ dhatte sakhedā varṣa |
sākrāntā jaghanasthalena guruṇā gantuṃ na śaktā vayaṃ
doṣairanyajanāśritairapaṭavo jātāḥ sma ityadbhutaṃ” ||
asyākhāpavādavādekadeśaskhayorvirodhe virodhālaṅkāraḥ |
(720) guṇaiḥ kriye vā yasyātāṃ viruddhe cetukāryyayoḥ |
yadā''rabdhasya vaiphalyamanarthasya ca sambhavaḥ ||
daśamaḥ parichedaḥ |
virūpayoḥ saṅghaṭanā yā ca tadviṣamaṃ mataṃ |
krameṇa yathā |
“sadyaḥ karasparśamavāpya citraṃ
rathe raṇe casya kṛpāṇalekhā |
tamālanīlā śaradindupāṇḍu
yaśastrilokābharaṇaṃ prakhate" ||
atra kāraṇarūpāsilatāyā: "kāraṇaguṇā hi kāryyaguṇa-
mārabhanne” iti khiterviruddhā śaklayajama utpatiḥ |
"śrāmandamamandamimaṃ kuvalayadalalocane dadāsi |
virahalayaiva janitastāpayatitarāṃ śarīraṃ me” ||
atrānandajanakastrīrūpakāraṇāttāpajanakavira hotpattiḥ |
“śrayaṃ ratnākaro'mbhodhirityasevi dhanāśayā |
dhanaṃ dūre'stu vadanamapūri cāravāribhiḥ” ||
atra kevalaṃ kācitadhanalābho nābhūt pratyuta cāravāribhi
rthadanapūraṇaṃ |
"ka vanaṃ taravaskabhūṣaṇaṃ nṛpalackīḥ ka mahendravanditā |
niyataṃ pratikūlavarttimā vata dhātucaritaṃ suduḥkhaī" |
atra vanarājaśriyorvirūpayo: sāṭanā | idaṃ mama | yathā vā |
" vipukhema sāgarajayastha kukṣiṇā
bhuvanāni casya papire yugaye |
madavibhramāsakalayā pape punaḥ
ma purastriyaikatamayaikavā dṛśā ||"
sāhibadaghaṃye |
(721) samaṃ syādānurūpyeca jhāṣā mogyasya vastunaḥ |
pacā|
"jinamupagatecaṃ kaumudī metramutraṃ
jalanidhimanurūpaṃ ahukanyā'vatīṣī |
iti samagupayogaprītayastaca paurā:
avaṇakaṭu nṛpālāmekavākyaṃ vivaśuḥ” ||
(752) vicitraṃ tadviruddhasya kṛtiriṣṭaphalāya cet ||
yathā |
"prathamatyuṣati he torjīvana hetorvimuñcati prācām |
duḥkhīyati sukhahetoḥ ko mūḍhaḥ sevakādanyaḥ” ||

“yugāntakālaprati saṃhatātmano

bathā|

mo| nāyikayā ca pratyuta sahagamane tato'pi sokaryeṇa hetu-

(735) praśnādapranato vāpi kathitādastuno bhavet |
tādṛganyavyapocañcecchābda ārtho'thavā tadā ||
parisaṃkhyā
krameṇodāharaṇaṃ |
“kiṃ bhūṣaṇaṃ sudṛḍhamatra yaśo na rabaṃ
kiṃ kāryyamārthacaritaṃ sukṛtaṃ na doṣaḥ |

atra vyavacchecaṃ razādi śābdaṃ |
“kimārādhyaṃ sadā puṣyaṃ kaca mevyaḥ sadāgamaḥ |
ko dhyeyo bhagavān viṣṇuḥ kiṃ kāyaṃ paramaṃ padaṃ ||"
atra vyavacche pāpācāyeṃ| anyo: praśnapūrvvakalaṃ | apraśna-

atra "pāyādapāyāditi” yamakaṃ | “saṃsāreti" śrādī
cānuprāsa iti śabdālaṅkārayoḥ saṃsṛṣṭi: | dvitīye pāde upamā
dvitīyā rūpakamityarthīlaṅkārayoḥ saṃsṛṣṭiḥ | ubhayoḥ
sthitatvācchabdā'rthālaṅkārasaṃsṛṣṭiḥ
(757) aṅgāṅgitve'laṅkṛtīnāṃ tadekāsthitā |
sandigdhatve ca bhavati saṅkara slividhaḥ punaḥ |
sāhityadarpaye |
prāṅgibhāvo yathā |
“ācaṣṭavegavimalagujagendrabhoga-
nikapaṭṭapariveṣṭanabāburāśeḥ |
manyavyathāvyupatramārthamivāśa yatra
mandākinī ciramaveṣṭata pādamūkhe” |
atra nikapaṭṭāpahavena mandākinyā bhāropa ityapahutiḥ |
sā ca mandākinyā vastuhantena yatpādamūlaveṣṭanaṃ tacaraphyūla-
veṣṭanamiti śleṣamutyāpayatīti tasyāṅgaṃ | dveṣañca pādamūlaveṣṭanameva |
caraṇamūlaveṣṭanamityatijayopheraṅgaṃ, atiśyokkica “manyavyathā-
vyupatramārthamiva” ityutprecāyā aṅgaṃ | utprecā cāmburāśimandāki-
nyonīyakanāyikāvyavahāraṃ gamayatīti samāsoraṅgaṃ | yathā vā |
"anurāgavatī sandhyā divasastatpuraḥsaraḥ |
aho daivagaticitrā tathāpi na samāgamaḥ” ||
atra samāsotirviśeṣakara | sandehamaro yathā |
“idamābhāti gagane bhindānaṃ santataṃ tamaḥ |
amandanabanānandakaraṃ maṇḍala maindavaṃ" ||
atra kiṃ mukhasya candratayādhyavasānādatiśayoktiḥ, uta
idamiti murkha niśyi candratvāropāṭrpakaṃ, atha vā idamiti
mukhaya candramaṇḍalasya ca iyorapi prakṛtayorekadharmābhisamma -
nyātukhyayogitā, āhokhicandra sāprakṛtalāhīpakaṃ, kiṃ vā
viśeṣaṇa sāmyāda prastutasya mukhassa gambalāt samāseoktiḥ, vadā
'prastuta candravardhana yā prastuta mukhāvagatiritya prastutapraśaṃsā,
daśamaḥ parichedaḥ |
yahnā manmathoddīpanaḥ kālaḥ svakāryyabhūta candravarṇanāmukhena varṇita
iti payāyotiriti bahamāmalaṅkārāṇāṃ sandehāt sandeha-
saṅkaraḥ, yathā vā mukhacandraṃ payāmītyatra kiṃ mukhaṃ candra ive-
tyupamā uta candra eveti rūpakamiti sandehaḥ, sādhakabādhakayo-
īyorekatarasya sadbhāvena punarna mandehaḥ | yathā "mukhacandraṃ cumba-
ti" ityatra cumbanaṃ mukhasyānukūla mityupamāyāḥ sādhakaṃ | candrasya
tu pratikūlamiti rūpakasya vādhakaṃ, "mukhacandra : prakāśate"
ityatra prakāśākhyo dharmo rūpakasya sādhako mukhe upacaritatvena
sambhavatīti nopamāvādhakaḥ |
“rājanārāyaṇaṃ lakṣmīlā māliṅgati nirbharaṃ” |
aba yoṣita prāliṅgamaṃ nāyakasya sadṛśeneocitamiti lakṣyā-
liṅganasya rājanyasambhavādupamāvādhakaṃ narāyaṇe sambhavādrūpakaṃ |
“evaṃ vadanāmbujameṇācyā bhāti cañcalalocamaṃ” |
atra vadane locanasya sambhavādupamāyā : bādhakatā, ambuje
cāsambhavādrūpakasya vādhakatā| evaṃ “sundaraṃ vadanāmbujaṃ” ityādau
sādhāraṇadharmma prayoge, “upamitaṃ vyāghrādibhiḥ sāmānyāprayoge”
iti vacanādupamāsamāso na sambhavatīti upamāyā vādhakaḥ |
evañcātra mayūravyaṃsakāditvādrūpakasamāsa eva| ekāayānupraveśo
yathā mama |
“kaṭāśeṇāpīvat caṇamapi nirīkṣeta yadi sā
tadānandaḥ sāndraḥ sphurati pihitāśeṣaviṣayaḥ |
saromāñcoda ñcatkucakalaśanirbhiśravasanaḥ
x 2
sāhityadarpaye|
parīrabhārāḥ ka iva bhavitākośaḥ” ||
“kaṭācecāpīvatvaramapi” ityaca dekānuprāsaca “birī-
ceta" ityatra cakāramādāya vṛtyanuprāsala caikāśraye'nupraveśaḥ |
evaṃ cācaivānuprāsārthāpatyalaṅkārayoḥ | yathā vā | “saṃsāradhyā-
nāvidhvaṃsa iti" ca rūpakānuprāsayoḥ | yathā vā | "kuravakā
ravakāracatāṃ yayuriti” atra ravakāravakā ityekaṃ vakāravakāra
ityekamiti camakadoḥ | yathā vā |
“ahiṣaapatrocarasīesa pavitra sāmāiesa diasesu |
soharū pakhāriagītrāṇaṃ pacitra moravindāṇaṃ” *
catra "paritra bāmāiesu" ityekātra ye pacika bhvāmāyi-
tetyupamā | patrikabāmājikeṣviti rūpakaṃ praviṣṭaṃ ||
śrīcandraśekharamahākavicandragu
śrīviśvanā thakavirājakataṃ prabandhaṃ |
sāhityadarpacamamuṃ sudhiyo vilokya
mārityadalamakhilaṃ sukhameva vitta ||
bāvatprasakhendunibhānanāśrīnārāyaṇasyāṅgamalaṅgharoti |
tāvanmana: sampadayam kavīnāmeṣa prabandhaḥ prathito'stu loke ||
iti sāhityadarpaye daśamaḥ paricchedaḥ samāptaḥ ||
samāptañcedaṃ sāhityadarpaṇaṃ ||
oṃ tatsat ||
* abhinavapayodharaśīteṣu pathika śyāmāyiteṣu divaseṣu |
śobhate prasāritagrīvāba nṛtyaṃ mayūrahandānāmiti || saṃ0 || ṭī0 ||
